99	function parseTypeCond(cond) {
100	  const res = { include: new Set(), hasInclude: false, exclude: new Set(), lt: null, lte: null, gt: null, gte: null };
101	  if (!cond || cond === 'else') return res;
102	  let s = cond;
103	  let m;
104	  const range = [
105	    [/type\s*>=\s*(\d+)\s*&&\s*type\s*<=\s*(\d+)/g, (a, b) => [a, b]],
106	    [/type\s*>\s*(\d+)\s*&&\s*type\s*<\s*(\d+)/g, (a, b) => [a + 1, b - 1]],
107	    [/type\s*>=\s*(\d+)\s*&&\s*type\s*<\s*(\d+)/g, (a, b) => [a, b - 1]],
108	  ];
109	  for (const [re, f] of range) {
110	    for (const mm of s.matchAll(re)) {
111	      const [a, b] = f(+mm[1], +mm[2]);
112	      for (let t = a; t <= b; t++) res.include.add(t);
113	      res.hasInclude = true;
114	    }
115	    s = s.replace(new RegExp(re.source, 'g'), ' ');
116	  }
117	  for (const mm of s.matchAll(/type\s*==\s*(-?\d+)/g)) { res.include.add(+mm[1]); res.hasInclude = true; }
118	  for (const mm of s.matchAll(/type\s*!=\s*(-?\d+)/g)) res.exclude.add(+mm[1]);
119	  if ((m = s.match(/type\s*<\s*(\d+)/))) res.lt = +m[1];
120	  if ((m = s.match(/type\s*<=\s*(\d+)/))) res.lte = +m[1];
121	  if ((m = s.match(/type\s*>\s*(\d+)/))) res.gt = +m[1];
122	  if ((m = s.match(/type\s*>=\s*(\d+)/)) && +m[1] !== 0) res.gte = +m[1];
123	  return res;
124	}

126	function parseLife(cond) {
127	  if (!cond || cond === 'else') return null;
128	  if (/life\s*<=\s*0/.test(cond)) return 'death';
129	  if (/life\s*>\s*0/.test(cond) || /life\s*>=\s*1/.test(cond)) return 'alive';
130	  return null;
131	}
132	
133	/** `ai[N] == V` / `ai[N] != V` → 结构化门 */
134	function parseAiGates(cond) {
135	  if (!cond) return [];
136	  const out = [];
137	  for (const m of cond.matchAll(/ai\[(\d)\]\s*==\s*(-?[\d.]+)[fF]?/g)) out.push({ slot: +m[1], value: +m[2], not: false });
138	  for (const m of cond.matchAll(/ai\[(\d)\]\s*!=\s*(-?[\d.]+)[fF]?/g)) out.push({ slot: +m[1], value: +m[2], not: true });
139	  return out;

142	/** 剥掉 type/life/ai 谓词后剩余标识符 → 附加门（专家模式/变体等），无则 null */
143	function parseGate(cond) {
144	  if (!cond || cond === 'else') return null;
145	  let s = cond;
146	  s = s.replace(/type\s*(==|!=|>=|<=|>|<)\s*(-?\d+|\w+(\.\w+)?)/g, ' ');
147	  s = s.replace(/life\s*(<=|>=|==|!=|>|<)\s*[\d.]+[fFdD]?/g, ' ');
148	  s = s.replace(/ai\[\d\]\s*(==|!=)\s*[\d.]+[fF]?/g, ' ');

150	  s = s.replace(/[\d.]+[fFdD]?/g, ' ').replace(/[()[\]{}&|!?:.,;=+\-*/<>]/g, ' ');
151	  const ids = [...new Set(s.split(/\s+/).filter((t) => /^[A-Za-z_]/.test(t)))];
152	  return ids.length ? ids.join('+') : null;
153	}

205	    if (!buf) buf = { text: src[ln], line: ln + 1 };
206	    else buf.text += ' ' + src[ln].trim();
207	    if (parenDepth(buf.text) <= 0) { logical.push(buf); buf = null; }
208	  }
209	  if (buf) logical.push(buf);
210	}
211	
212	const stack = [];       // 深度栈：每项一个守卫上下文
213	let depth = 0;
214	let ctxSeq = 0;
215	let pendingGuard = null;
216	const caseAt = new Map(); // depth → { label, types:Set|null }
217	const locals = new Map(); // name → [{ line, valExpr, typeCtxId }]
218	
219	const entries = new Map();
220	const defaultEntries = [];
221	const unresolved = [];
222	let callsTotal = 0, callsHitSkipped = 0, callsPartySkipped = 0;
223	
224	function makeGuard(kind, cond) {
225	  const t = parseTypeCond(cond);
226	  return {
227	    kind, cond,
228	    types: t.hasInclude ? t.include : null,
229	    exclude: t.exclude.size ? t.exclude : null,
230	    bounds: { lt: t.lt, lte: t.lte, gt: t.gt, gte: t.gte },
231	    life: parseLife(cond),
232	    gate: parseGate(cond),
233	    ai: parseAiGates(cond),
234	  };
235	}
236	function pushCtx(guard) {
237	  ctxSeq++;
238	  stack.push({
239	    id: ctxSeq, kind: guard?.kind ?? null, cond: guard?.cond ?? null,
240	    types: guard?.types ?? null, exclude: guard?.exclude ?? null,
241	    bounds: guard?.bounds ?? null, life: guard?.life ?? null,
242	    gate: guard?.gate ?? null, ai: guard?.ai ?? null,
243	    loopN: guard?.loopN ?? null, loopVar: guard?.loopVar ?? null,
244	  });
245	}
246	/** 调用点守卫链解析 */
247	function resolveChain(extraCase) {
248	  const chain = [...stack];
249	  if (extraCase) chain.push(extraCase);
250	  let include = null, typeCtxId = null;
251	  const exclude = new Set(), gates = [], ai = [];
252	  for (const c of chain) {
253	    if (!c) continue;
254	    if (c.types) {
255	      include = include ? new Set([...include].filter((t) => c.types.has(t))) : new Set(c.types);
256	      typeCtxId = typeCtxId ?? c.id;
257	    }
258	    if (c.exclude) for (const t of c.exclude) exclude.add(t);
259	    if (c.gate) gates.push(c.gate);
260	    if (c.ai) ai.push(...c.ai);
261	    if (c.bounds && include) {
262	      if (c.bounds.lt != null) include = new Set([...include].filter((t) => t < c.bounds.lt));
263	      if (c.bounds.lte != null) include = new Set([...include].filter((t) => t <= c.bounds.lte));
264	      if (c.bounds.gt != null) include = new Set([...include].filter((t) => t > c.bounds.gt));
265	      if (c.bounds.gte != null) include = new Set([...include].filter((t) => t >= c.bounds.gte));
266	    }
267	  }
268	  let life = null;
269	  for (let i = chain.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) if (chain[i]?.life) { life = chain[i].life; break; }
270	  let loopN = 1;
271	  const loopVars = [];
272	  for (const c of chain) {
273	    if (c?.loopN) loopN *= c.loopN;
274	    if (c?.loopVar) loopVars.push(c.loopVar);
275	  }
276	  return { types: include, exclude, life, gates, ai, typeCtxId, loopN, loopVars };
277	}
278	
279	function recordLocalAssign(name, valExpr, line, typeCtxId, loopRange) {
280	  if (!locals.has(name)) locals.set(name, []);
281	  locals.get(name).push({ line, valExpr, typeCtxId, loopRange });
282	}
283	
284	// ---- 简易算术求值（局部变量/`type` 代数 id 还原用）----
285	/** 递归下降：数 / 一元± / * / + -；括号。`type==N` 归一后为 0/1。返回 number | null */
286	function evalArith(expr) {
287	  const toks = [];
288	  let s = String(expr).trim();
289	  let m;
290	  while (s.length) {
291	    if (/^\s/.test(s)) { s = s.slice(1); continue; }
292	    if ((m = s.match(/^(-?\d+\.?\d*)/))) { toks.push({ t: 'n', v: +m[1] }); s = s.slice(m[1].length); continue; }
293	    if ((m = s.match(/^([-+*()/<>?:])/))) {
294	      const op = m[1];
295	      // 双字符 <= >= == != 跳过（归一阶段已替换成数字）
296	      if ((op === '<' || op === '>') && '=<>=!'.includes(s[1] ?? '')) return null;
297	      if ((op === ':' || op === '?') && toks.length) { /* 三元（cond 已归一为数）：手工处理 */ }
298	      toks.push({ t: op }); s = s.slice(1); continue;
299	    }
300	    if ((m = s.match(/^==/))) return null;
301	    return null;
302	  }
303	  let pos = 0;
304	  const peek = () => toks[pos];
305	  const eat = (t) => { if (toks[pos] && toks[pos].t === t) { pos++; return true; } return false; };
306	  function ternary() {
307	    const c = additive();
308	    if (c == null) return null;
309	    if (eat('?')) {
310	      const a = ternary();
311	      if (a == null || !eat(':')) return null;
312	      const b = ternary();
313	      if (b == null) return null;
314	      return c !== 0 ? a : b;
315	    }
316	    return c;
317	  }
318	  function additive() {
319	    let v = multiplicative();
320	    while (v != null && (peek() && (peek().t === '+' || peek().t === '-'))) {
321	      const op = peek().t; pos++;
322	      const r = multiplicative();
323	      if (r == null) return null;
324	      v = op === '+' ? v + r : v - r;
325	    }
326	    return v;
327	  }
328	  function multiplicative() {
329	    let v = unary();
330	    while (v != null && peek() && peek().t === '*') {
331	      pos++;
332	      const r = unary();
333	      if (r == null) return null;
334	      v = v * r;
335	    }
336	    return v;
337	  }
338	  function unary() {
339	    if (eat('-')) { const v = unary(); return v == null ? null : -v; }
340	    if (eat('+')) return unary();
341	    if (eat('(')) { const v = ternary(); return eat(')') ? v : null; }
342	    const t = peek();
343	    if (t && t.t === 'n') { pos++; return t.v; }
344	    return null;
345	  }
346	  const out = ternary();
347	  return pos === toks.length ? out : null;
348	}
349	
350	/** `type` → 具体值代入 + `X == Y` 归一为 0/1（仅支持全数字比较） */
351	function normalizeForArith(expr, t) {
352	  let s = String(expr).replace(/\btype\b/g, String(t));
353	  s = s.replace(/\(\s*(\d+)\s*==\s*(\d+)\s*\)/g, (_, a, b2) => (a === b2 ? '(1)' : '(0)'));
354	  s = s.replace(/(\d+)\s*==\s*(\d+)/g, (_, a, b2) => (a === b2 ? '1' : '0'));
355	  s = s.replace(/\(\s*\)/g, '');
356	  return s;
357	}
358	
359	/** 单 type 值下的 id 求值 → {kind:id|range|ids} | null */
360	function evalIdForType(expr, t, callLine, typeCtxId) {
361	  const e = evalIdExpr(expr);
362	  if (e) {
363	    if (e.kind === 'id' || e.kind === 'range' || e.kind === 'ids') return e;
364	    if (e.kind === 'local') {
365	      const base = localValueForType(e.name, t, callLine, typeCtxId);
366	      if (base == null) return null;
367	      return applyOff(base, e.off);
368	    }
369	    return null;
370	  }
371	  // `X + local` / `local + rand(N)` / `local ± K` 反向形态
372	  let m;
373	  if ((m = expr.match(/^(-?\d+)\s*\+\s*([A-Za-z_]\w*)$/))) {
374	    const base = localValueForType(m[2], t, callLine, typeCtxId);
375	    return base == null ? null : applyOff(base, +m[1]);
376	  }
377	  if ((m = expr.match(/^([A-Za-z_]\w*)\s*\+\s*Main\.rand\.Next\(\s*(\d+)\s*\)$/))) {
378	    const base = localValueForType(m[1], t, callLine, typeCtxId);
379	    if (base == null || base.kind !== 'id') return null;
380	    return { kind: 'range', min: base.id, max: base.id + +m[2] - 1 };
381	  }
382	  if ((m = expr.match(/^(-?\d+)\s*\+\s*\(\(Main\.rand\.Next\(\d+\)\s*==\s*0\)\s*\?\s*(-?\d+)\s*:\s*(-?\d+)\)$/))) {
383	    return { kind: 'ids', ids: [+m[1] + +m[2], +m[1] + +m[3]] };
384	  }
385	  // 含 type 的代数式：代入后算术求值
386	  if (/\btype\b/.test(expr)) {
387	    const v = evalArith(normalizeForArith(expr, t));
388	    if (v != null) return { kind: 'id', id: v };
389	  }
390	  return null;
391	}
392	
393	/** 局部变量在 type=t 下的值（赋值流末值；声明含 type/循环变量则代数求值） */
394	function localValueForType(name, t, callLine, typeCtxId) {
395	  const hist = (locals.get(name) ?? []).filter((h) => h.line < callLine && (h.typeCtxId === typeCtxId || h.typeCtxId == null));
396	  if (!hist.length) return null;
397	  const last = hist[hist.length - 1];
398	  if (last.loopRange) return { kind: 'range', min: last.loopRange[0], max: last.loopRange[1] };
399	  const e = evalIdExpr(last.valExpr);
400	  if (e && e.kind !== 'local') return e;
401	  if (/\btype\b/.test(last.valExpr)) {
402	    const v = evalArith(normalizeForArith(last.valExpr, t));
403	    if (v != null) return { kind: 'id', id: v };
404	  }
405	  if (/^([A-Za-z_]\w*)$/.test(last.valExpr.trim())) {
406	    return localValueForType(last.valExpr.trim(), t, callLine, typeCtxId);
407	  }
408	  // 算术组合（如 `11 + num744`，num744 为循环变量）
409	  let m;
410	  if ((m = last.valExpr.match(/^(-?\d+)\s*\+\s*([A-Za-z_]\w*)$/))) {
411	    const base = localValueForType(m[2], t, callLine, typeCtxId);
412	    return base == null ? null : applyOff(base, +m[1]);
413	  }
414	  return null;
415	}
416	
417	function applyOff(v, off) {
418	  if (v.kind === 'id') return { kind: 'id', id: v.id + off };
419	  if (v.kind === 'range') return { kind: 'range', min: v.min + off, max: v.max + off };
420	  if (v.kind === 'ids') return { kind: 'ids', ids: v.ids.map((x) => x + off) };
421	  return null;
422	}
423	
424	/** 逐 type 求值 → {fields} 统一 / {perType:Map} 分歧 / null 不可求值 */
425	function resolveIdPerType(expr, target, callLine, typeCtxId) {
426	  const per = new Map();
427	  for (const t of target) {
428	    const v = evalIdForType(expr, t, callLine, typeCtxId);
429	    if (!v) return null;
430	    per.set(t, v);
431	  }
432	  const uniq = [...new Set([...per.values()].map((v) => JSON.stringify(v)))];
433	  if (uniq.length === 1) return { fields: idFields(JSON.parse(uniq[0])) };
434	  const perMap = new Map();
435	  for (const [t, v] of per) perMap.set(t, idFields(v));
436	  return { perType: perMap };
437	}
438	
439	function idFields(e) {
440	  if (e.kind === 'id') return { id: e.id };
441	  if (e.kind === 'range') return { idMin: e.min, idMax: e.max, n: e.max - e.min + 1 };
442	  if (e.kind === 'ids') return { ids: e.ids, n: e.ids.length };
443	  return {};
444	}
445	
446	// ---- 位置/速度表达式 → 结构化数值（运行时只做一次线性组合）----
447	/** 单轴项求值 → { off, frac, rand } | null；axisDim = 'width' | 'height' */
448	function axisTerm(term, axisDim) {
449	  const t = term.trim();
450	  let m;
451	  if ((m = t.match(/^(-?[\d.]+)[fF]?$/))) return { off: +m[1] };
452	  if ((m = t.match(/^\(float\)\((width|height)\s*\/\s*(\d+)\)$/)) || (m = t.match(/^(width|height)\s*\/\s*(\d+)$/))) {
453	    return m[1] === axisDim ? { frac: 1 / +m[2] } : null;
454	  }
455	  if ((m = t.match(/^\(float\)(width|height)$/)) || (m = t.match(/^(width|height)$/))) {
456	    return m[1] === axisDim ? { frac: 1 } : null;
457	  }
458	  if ((m = t.match(/^\(?float\)?\s*Main\.rand\.Next\(\s*(width|height)\s*\)$/)) || (m = t.match(/^Main\.rand\.Next\(\s*(width|height)\s*\)$/))) {
459	    return m[1] === axisDim ? { rand: 1 } : null;
460	  }
461	  if ((m = t.match(/^(width|height)\s*\*\s*([\d.]+)[fF]?$/))) {
462	    return m[1] === axisDim ? { frac: +m[2] } : null;
463	  }
464	  return null;
465	}
466	
467	/** 轴表达式（已剥 anchor）→ { off, frac, rand } | null */
468	function axisExpr(expr, axisDim) {
469	  let off = 0, frac = 0, rand = 0;
470	  // 顶层 ± 切分（无括号嵌套数字场景）
471	  const parts = expr.replace(/^\s*\+/, '').split(/\s*\+\s*/);
472	  const terms = [];
473	  for (const p of parts) {
474	    const neg = p.split(/\s*-\s*/);
475	    for (let k = 0; k < neg.length; k++) {
476	      if (neg[k] === '') continue;
477	      terms.push({ t: neg[k], sign: k === 0 ? 1 : -1 });
478	    }
479	  }
480	  for (const { t, sign } of terms) {
481	    const r = axisTerm(t, axisDim);
482	    if (!r) return null;
483	    if (r.off != null) off += sign * r.off;
484	    if (r.frac != null) frac += sign * r.frac;
485	    if (r.rand != null) rand += sign * r.rand;
486	  }
487	  const out = {};
488	  if (off) out.off = +off.toFixed(4);
489	  if (frac) out.frac = +frac.toFixed(4);
490	  if (rand) out.rand = 1;
491	  return out;
492	}
493	
494	/** Position 实参 → { ox,oy,fx,fy,rx,ry } | null（基准=position 左上角） */
495	function compilePos(expr) {
496	  let s = String(expr).trim();
497	  let m;
498	  // anchor 前缀（base.Center/Top/Bottom/position + …）
499	  let base = {};
500	  if ((m = s.match(/^(base\.Center|base\.Top|base\.Bottom|position)\s*\+\s*(.+)$/s))) {
501	    if (m[1] === 'base.Center') base = { fx: 0.5, fy: 0.5 };
502	    else if (m[1] === 'base.Top') base = { fx: 0.5 };
503	    else if (m[1] === 'base.Bottom') base = { fx: 0.5, fy: 1 };
504	    s = m[2].trim();
505	  }
506	  // 实体参照归一到 position.X/Y + 尺寸项
507	  s = s.replace(/base\.Center\.X/g, 'position.X + (float)(width / 2)')
508	    .replace(/base\.Center\.Y/g, 'position.Y + (float)(height / 2)')
509	    .replace(/base\.Top\.X/g, 'position.X + (float)(width / 2)')
510	    .replace(/base\.Top\.Y/g, 'position.Y')
511	    .replace(/base\.Bottom\.X/g, 'position.X + (float)(width / 2)')
512	    .replace(/base\.Bottom\.Y/g, 'position.Y + (float)height')
513	    .replace(/base\.Right\.X/g, 'position.X + (float)width')
514	    .replace(/base\.Left\.X/g, 'position.X')
515	    .replace(/base\.Size\s*\*\s*0\.5f?/g, 'new Vector2((float)(width / 2), (float)(height / 2))');
516	  if (s === 'position' || s === '') return { ...base };
517	  if (s === 'base.Center') return { fx: 0.5, fy: 0.5 };
518	  if (s === 'base.Top') return { fx: 0.5 };
519	  if (s === 'base.Bottom') return { fx: 0.5, fy: 1 };
520	  let sx = null, sy = null;
521	  if (s.startsWith('new Vector2(')) {
522	    const comps = splitArgs(parenBody(s, s.indexOf('(')));
523	    if (comps.length !== 2) return null;
524	    sx = comps[0]; sy = comps[1];
525	  } else if ((m = s.match(/^(.*?)[+-]\s*new Vector2\(/s))) {
526	    return null; // 复合表达式（vector19 链等）→ 留原文
527	  } else {
528	    return null;
529	  }
530	  const out = { ...base };
531	  for (const [axisName, dim, comp] of [['x', 'width', sx], ['y', 'height', sy]]) {
532	    let body = comp.trim();
533	    const anchorRe = new RegExp(`position\\.${axisName.toUpperCase()}\\b`);
534	    if (!anchorRe.test(body)) {
535	      // 无 position 锚点：整体当作偏移项（含 width/height 项）
536	      const r = axisExpr(body, dim);
537	      if (!r) return null;
538	      if (r.off != null) out[`o${axisName}`] = r.off;
539	      if (r.frac != null) out[`f${axisName}`] = r.frac;
540	      if (r.rand) out[`r${axisName}`] = 1;
541	      continue;
542	    }
543	    body = body.replace(anchorRe, '');
544	    const r = axisExpr(body, dim);
545	    if (!r) return null;
546	    if (r.off != null) out[`o${axisName}`] = r.off;
547	    if (r.frac != null) out[`f${axisName}`] = r.frac;
548	    if (r.rand) out[`r${axisName}`] = 1;
549	  }
550	  return out;
551	}
552	
553	/** 速度实参 → { vx,vy,kx,ky,ax,ay,mx,my,hd } | null（k=NPC 速度系数；
554	 *  v=常量；m+a=均匀随机区间 [m-a, m+a]；hd=1 表示 vx 取击退方向） */
555	function compileVel(expr) {
556	  const s = String(expr).trim();
557	  let m;
558	  if (s === 'velocity') return {};
559	  if (s === 'Vector2.Zero' || s === 'default(Vector2)') return { vx: 0, vy: 0 };
560	  if ((m = s.match(/^velocity\s*\*\s*([\d.]+)[fF]?$/))) return +m[1] === 0 ? { vx: 0, vy: 0 } : { kx: +m[1], ky: +m[1] };
561	  if (!s.startsWith('new Vector2(')) return null;
562	  const comps = splitArgs(parenBody(s, s.indexOf('(')));
563	  if (comps.length !== 2) return null;
564	  const out = {};
565	  const comp = (c, axis) => {
566	    const t = c.trim();
567	    let mm;
568	    if ((mm = t.match(/^(-?[\d.]+)[fF]?$/))) { out[`v${axis}`] = +mm[1]; return true; }
569	    if (t === 'hitDirection') { out.hd = 1; return true; }
570	    if ((mm = t.match(/^\(float\)\s*Main\.rand\.Next\((-?\d+),\s*(-?\d+)\)\s*\*\s*([\d.]+)[fF]?$/))
571	      || (mm = t.match(/^Main\.rand\.Next\((-?\d+),\s*(-?\d+)\)\s*\*\s*([\d.]+)[fF]?$/))) {
572	      const lo = +mm[1] * +mm[3], hi = (+mm[2] - 1) * +mm[3];
573	      out[`m${axis}`] = +((lo + hi) / 2).toFixed(4);
574	      out[`a${axis}`] = +((hi - lo) / 2).toFixed(4);
575	      return true;
576	    }
577	    if ((mm = t.match(/^Main\.rand\.Next\((-?\d+),\s*(-?\d+)\)$/))) {
578	      const lo = +mm[1], hi = +mm[2] - 1;
579	      out[`m${axis}`] = +((lo + hi) / 2).toFixed(4);
580	      out[`a${axis}`] = +((hi - lo) / 2).toFixed(4);
581	      return true;
582	    }
583	    if ((mm = t.match(/^Main\.rand\.Next\((-?\d+)\)$/))) {
584	      out[`m${axis}`] = +((+mm[1] - 1) / 2).toFixed(4);
585	      out[`a${axis}`] = +((+mm[1] - 1) / 2).toFixed(4);
586	      return true;
587	    }
588	    if ((mm = t.match(/^Main\.rand\.NextFloat\(\)\s*\*\s*([\d.]+)[fF]?$/))) {
589	      out[`m${axis}`] = +((+mm[1]) / 2).toFixed(4);
590	      out[`a${axis}`] = +((+mm[1]) / 2).toFixed(4);
591	      return true;
592	    }
593	    return false;
594	  };
595	  if (!comp(comps[0], 'x') || !comp(comps[1], 'y')) return null;
596	  return out;
597	}
598	
599	function emitEntry(npcId, fields, line, gates, ai, loopN) {
600	  if (!entries.has(npcId)) entries.set(npcId, []);
601	  const f = { ...fields, line };
602	  if (gates.length) f.gates = gates;
603	  if (ai.length) f.ai = ai;
604	  if (loopN && loopN > 1) f.loop = loopN;
605	  entries.get(npcId).push(f);
606	}
607	
608	// ---------- 逻辑行扫描（词法括号走查：对象初始化器 `{`/尾随 `}` 也计 depth） ----------
609	const LOOP_GUARD = { kind: 'loop', cond: null, types: null, exclude: null, bounds: null, life: null, gate: null, ai: null };
610	for (const { text, line: lineNo } of logical) {
611	  let i = 0;
612	  let guard = pendingGuard;   // 跨行：守卫头与 `{` 分行的挂起上下文
613	  pendingGuard = null;
614	
615	  const plain = text.trim();
616	  // 局部变量赋值流（声明与重赋值；供 `num + K` id 还原）
617	  {
618	    let m;
619	    if ((m = plain.match(/^(?:int|float|double|byte|short|long|uint|var|bool|Vector2)\s+([A-Za-z_]\w*)\s*=\s*(.+?);$/))
620	      || (m = plain.match(/^([A-Za-z_]\w*)\s*=(?![=>])\s*(.+?);$/))) {
621	      if (!/^(if|for|while|switch|return|else)$/.test(m[1])) {
622	        const r = resolveChain(null);
623	        recordLocalAssign(m[1], m[2], lineNo, r.typeCtxId);
624	      }
625	    }
626	  }
627	
628	  while (i < text.length) {
629	    const c = text[i];
630	    if (c === '{') {
631	      pushCtx(guard ?? null);
632	      guard = null;
633	      depth++;
634	      i++;
635	      continue;
636	    }
637	    if (c === '}') {
638	      if (stack.length) stack.pop();
639	      depth--;
640	      for (const k of [...caseAt.keys()]) if (k > depth) caseAt.delete(k);
641	      guard = null;
642	      i++;
643	      continue;
644	    }
645	    if (c === ' ' || c === '\t' || c === ';') { i++; continue; }
646	
647	    // 语句起点：守卫头 / case 标签 / 普通语句段
648	    const rest = text.slice(i);
649	    let m;
650	    let handled = true;
651	    if ((m = rest.match(/^(else\s+if|if)\s*\(/))) {
652	      const cond = parenBody(rest, m[0].length - 1);
653	      guard = makeGuard(m[1].replace(/\s+/g, ''), cond);
654	      i += m[0].length + cond.length + 1;
655	    } else if ((m = rest.match(/^else\b/))) {
656	      guard = makeGuard('else', 'else');
657	      i += m[0].length;
658	    } else if ((m = rest.match(/^switch\s*\(/))) {
659	      const cond = parenBody(rest, m[0].length - 1);
660	      guard = makeGuard('switch', cond);
661	      i += m[0].length + cond.length + 1;
662	    } else if ((m = rest.match(/^case\s+([^:]+):/))) {
663	      const lbl = m[1].trim();
664	      const e = evalIdExpr(lbl);
665	      caseAt.set(depth, { label: lbl, types: e && e.kind === 'id' ? new Set([e.id]) : null });
666	      i += m[0].length;
667	    } else if ((m = rest.match(/^default\s*:/))) {
668	      caseAt.set(depth, null);
669	      i += m[0].length;
670	    } else if ((m = rest.match(/^(for|foreach|while|do|try)\b/))) {
671	      // for 头部：整数循环变量区间（id 表达式引用时还原）+ 常量次数（表项 loop 字段，
672	      // 运行时按次重复生成——EoC `for(num579<2)` 内 4 调用 = 8 块）
673	      const fh = rest.match(/^for\s*\(\s*(?:int|float|double|var)?\s*([A-Za-z_]\w*)\s*=\s*(-?\d+)\s*;\s*\1\s*(<=|<)\s*(\d+)\s*;/);
674	      let loopN = 1;
675	      if (fh) {
676	        const lo = +fh[2], hi = +fh[4];
677	        const rg = [lo, fh[3] === '<=' ? hi : hi - 1];
678	        recordLocalAssign(fh[1], `${rg[0]}`, lineNo, resolveChain(null).typeCtxId, rg);
679	        const n = (fh[3] === '<=' ? hi - lo + 1 : hi - lo);
680	        if (n > 0 && n <= 64) loopN = n;
681	        guard = { ...LOOP_GUARD, loopN, loopVar: fh[1] };
682	      }
683	      // 头部条件括号整体跳过（否则会被当成语句段消费掉守卫）
684	      const op = rest.indexOf('(');
685	      if (op >= 0) {
686	        const hd = parenBody(rest, op);
687	        i += op + hd.length + 2;
688	      } else {
689	        i += m[0].length;
690	      }
691	    } else {
692	      handled = false;
693	    }
694	    if (handled) continue;
695	
696	    // 普通语句段：吃到顶层 ; { } 为止；段内提取 Gore.NewGore* 调用（此时 depth 即调用点深度）
697	    let j = i, pd = 0;
698	    const segStart = i;
699	    while (j < text.length) {
700	      const ch = text[j];
701	      if (ch === '(') pd++;
702	      else if (ch === ')') pd--;
703	      else if (pd === 0 && (ch === ';' || ch === '{' || ch === '}')) break;
704	      j++;
705	    }
706	    const seg = text.slice(segStart, j);
707	    const segLoopN = guard && guard.kind === 'loop' ? (guard.loopN ?? 1) : 1;
708	    let from = 0;
709	    for (;;) {
710	      const at = seg.indexOf('Gore.NewGore', from);
711	      if (at < 0) break;
712	      from = at + 1;
713	      const p = seg.indexOf('(', at);
714	      if (p < 0) continue;
715	      const callee = seg.slice(at, p);
716	      if (!/^Gore\.NewGore(Perfect|Direct)?$/.test(callee)) continue;
717	      handleGoreCall(callee, splitArgs(parenBody(seg, p)), lineNo, segLoopN);
718	    }
719	    if (guard && guard.kind === 'loop') guard = null; // 无括号体 = 单语句，守卫就此消费
720	    i = j;
721	  }
722	  // 行尾仍未消费的守卫头（与 `{` 分行）挂到下一逻辑行
723	  if (guard) pendingGuard = guard;
724	}
725	
726	/** 单个 Gore.NewGore* 调用 → 表项 */
727	function handleGoreCall(callee, args, lineNo, segLoopN = 1) {
728	  callsTotal++;
729	  const a = namedArgs(args);
730	  if (a.Position === undefined || a.Type === undefined) return;
731	  const cc = caseAt.get(depth) ?? caseAt.get(depth - 1) ?? null;
732	  if (process.env.GORE_DBG_LINE && lineNo === +process.env.GORE_DBG_LINE) {
733	    console.error('[dbg] line', lineNo, 'depth', depth, 'stack', JSON.stringify(stack.map((c2) => c2?.cond)), 'case', cc?.label ?? null);
734	  }
735	  const r = resolveChain(cc ? { id: 0, kind: 'case', cond: `case ${cc.label}`, types: cc.types, exclude: null, bounds: null, life: null, gate: null, ai: null, loopN: null, loopVar: null } : null);
736	  r.loopN *= segLoopN;
737	
738	  if (r.gates.some((g) => g.includes('UsesPartyHat'))) { callsPartySkipped++; return; } // 派对帽专属
739	  if (r.life === 'alive') { callsHitSkipped++; return; }                               // hit 段
740	
741	  const velFields = (() => {
742	    const v = String(a.Velocity ?? 'velocity');
743	    const c = compileVel(v);
744	    if (c) return c;
745	    return { velExpr: v }; // 不可编译 → 运行时按继承 NPC 速度
746	  })();
747	  const scaleFields = (() => {
748	    const v = a.Scale;
749	    if (v === undefined || v === '1f' || v === '1') return {};
750	    let m;
751	    if ((m = String(v).match(/^([\d.]+)[fF]?$/))) return { scale: +m[1] };
752	    return { scaleExpr: String(v) }; // 'scale' = NPC.scale；其余登记原文
753	  })();
754	  const posFields = (() => {
755	    if (a.Position === 'position') return {};
756	    const c = compilePos(a.Position);
757	    if (c) return c;
758	    return { posExpr: String(a.Position) }; // 不可编译 → 运行时按左上角
759	  })();
760	  const perfect = callee === 'Gore.NewGorePerfect' ? { perfect: true } : {};
761	  const base = { ...velFields, ...scaleFields, ...posFields, ...perfect };
762	
763	  // id 表达式直接引用外层循环变量（如 `num343`）= 每次迭代取一个具体 id，
764	  // 区间/候选已编码迭代序列 → loop 归 1，防运行时重复随机取样
765	  if (r.loopN > 1 && r.loopVars.some((v) => new RegExp(`\\b${v}\\b`).test(String(a.Type)))) r.loopN = 1;
766	
767	  let e = null;
768	  let perType = null;
769	  let fields = null;
770	
771	  if (r.types) {
772	    const target = [...r.types].filter((t) => !r.exclude.has(t));
773	    const res = resolveIdPerType(String(a.Type), target, lineNo, r.typeCtxId);
774	    if (res?.fields) fields = res.fields;
775	    else if (res?.perType) perType = res.perType;
776	    // 区间/候选尺寸 == 循环次数 = 序列已被区间编码（469-475×7 等）→ loop 归 1
777	    const span = fields ? (fields.ids ? fields.ids.length : fields.idMin != null ? (fields.idMax - fields.idMin + 1) : 0) : 0;
778	    if (span > 1 && span === r.loopN) r.loopN = 1;
779	  }
780	
781	  if (!r.types) {
782	    defaultEntries.push({ line: lineNo, idExpr: String(a.Type), gates: r.gates });
783	    return;
784	  }
785	  const target = [...r.types].filter((t) => !r.exclude.has(t));
786	
787	  if (perType) {
788	    for (const t of target) {
789	      emitEntry(t, { ...base, ...perType.get(t) }, lineNo, r.gates, r.ai, r.loopN);
790	    }
791	    return;
792	  }
793	  if (fields) {
794	    for (const t of target) emitEntry(t, { ...base, ...fields }, lineNo, r.gates, r.ai, r.loopN);
795	    return;
796	  }
797	  for (const t of target) emitEntry(t, { ...base, expr: String(a.Type) }, lineNo, r.gates, r.ai, r.loopN);
798	  unresolved.push({
799	    line: lineNo, expr: String(a.Type),
800	    types: target.length > 6 ? `${target[0]}..${target[target.length - 1]}(${target.length})` : target.join('|'),
801	  });
802	}
803	
804	// ============ 手工补丁：守卫链为排除式(`type != N` + return)的收尾支 ============
805	// catch-all else 尾支（NPC.cs:90859-90867）：`if (type != 156) return;` → 仅 Red Devil
806	// 走到 184/185/185。排除式守卫无法从链上还原出正集（需要整链 else 语义），按源行手工钉死。
807	const MANUAL_PATCHES = {
808	  156: [
809	    { id: 184, line: 90865 },
810	    { id: 185, line: 90866 },
811	    { id: 185, line: 90867 },
812	  ],
813	};
814	for (const [npcId, list] of Object.entries(MANUAL_PATCHES)) {
815	  if (!entries.has(+npcId)) entries.set(+npcId, []);
816	  entries.get(+npcId).push(...list);
817	}
818	
819	// ============ 贴图尺寸表（出现过的 gore id） ============
820	const idsSeen = new Set();
821	for (const list of entries.values()) {
822	  for (const e of list) {
823	    if (e.id != null) idsSeen.add(e.id);
824	    if (e.idMin != null) for (let i = e.idMin; i <= (e.idMax ?? e.idMin); i++) idsSeen.add(i);
825	    if (e.ids) for (const i of e.ids) idsSeen.add(i);
826	  }
827	}
828	const tex = {};
829	for (const id of [...idsSeen].sort((a, b) => a - b)) {
830	  const sz = pngSize(id);
831	  if (sz) tex[id] = sz;
832	}
833	
834	// ============ 落盘 ============
835	const entryCount = [...entries.values()].reduce((n, l) => n + l.length, 0);
836	const out = {
837	  _meta: {
838	    source: 'Terarria1456/Terraria/NPC.cs HitEffect (:82421-:90873)；物理=Terraria/Gore.cs',
839	    generatedBy: 'tools/extract-gore.mjs',

842	    callsPartySkipped,
843	    npcIds: entries.size,
844	    entryCount,
845	    unresolvedCount: unresolved.length,
846	    defaultEntryCount: defaultEntries.length,
847	    unresolved,
848	    defaultEntries: defaultEntries.slice(0, 40),
849	    notes: [
850	      'life>0 守卫段(hit gore)与派对帽段(UsesPartyHat)已剔除；无 life 守卫的调用原版 hit/death 双段都执行 → 计为死亡 gore',
851	      'idMin..idMax = 原版 X+Next(N)/Next(A,B) 区间，运行时均匀取值；ids = SelectRandom 候选',
852	      'velocity 缺省=继承 NPC 当前速度；NewGore 附加随机扰动见 Gore.cs:1347-1348（运行时实现）',
853	      'scaleExpr="scale" = 取 NPC.scale；posExpr 为原版位置表达式（默认 position=左上角）',
854	      'ai=[{slot,value}] = 原版 ai[N]==V 门（运行时按 NPC ai 槽位判定，不满足跳过该条）',
855	      'perfect=true = Gore.NewGorePerfect：随机扰动后回写精确 pos/vel（运行时不抖动）',
856	      'loop=N = 原版常量次数 for 循环体内（运行时重复生成 N 次，如 EoC <2 循环 = 8 块）',
857	      'gates = 其余不可求值守卫（专家模式/变体等），仅登记不拦截',
858	      'tex = gore 贴图 {w,h}（IHDR 直读）；缺图 id 运行时回退 32px 碰撞盒（Gore.cs:999）',
859	    ],
860	    tex,
861	  },
862	};
863	for (const [k, v] of [...entries.entries()].sort((a, b) => (+a[0]) - (+b[0]))) out[k] = v;
864	
865	if (process.argv.includes('--audit')) {
866	  console.log(JSON.stringify({
867	    npcIds: entries.size, entryCount, callsTotal, callsHitSkipped, callsPartySkipped,
868	    unresolved: unresolved.length, defaultEntries: defaultEntries.length,
869	    texKnown: Object.keys(tex).length, texMissing: idsSeen.size - Object.keys(tex).length,
870	    sample: { 1: out[1], 3: out[3], 50: out[50] },
871	  }));
872	} else {
873	  writeFileSync(OUT, JSON.stringify(out, null, 1) + '\n');
874	  console.log(`vanilla-gore.json: ${entries.size} npcIds, ${entryCount} entries, `

113	  /** Gore.type（贴图 Gore_<type>.png） */
114	  readonly goreId: number;
115	  rotation = 0;
116	  scale: number;
117	  alpha = 0;
118	  timeLeft = GORE_TIME;
119	  sticky = true;
120	  /** 贴图原始尺寸（碰撞盒与绘制基准；缺失回退 32） */
121	  texW: number;
122	  texH: number;
123	  /** SpriteFrame 行（多帧竖条：1217/1218 等） */
124	  row = 0;
125	  private frameCounter = 0;
126	
127	  constructor(type: number, x: number, y: number, scale: number) {
128	    super();
129	    this.goreId = type;
130	    this.scale = scale;
131	    const tex = TEX[type];
132	    this.texW = tex?.w ?? FALLBACK_TEX;
133	    this.texH = tex?.h ?? FALLBACK_TEX;
134	    this.x = x;
135	    this.y = y;
136	    this.resizeBox();
137	  }
138	
139	  /** 碰撞盒 = min(texW,texH)*0.9*scale（Gore.cs:1000-1014） */
140	  private resizeBox() {
141	    const raw = Math.min(this.texW, this.texH) * 0.9 * this.scale;
142	    this.w = Math.max(2, Math.floor(raw));
143	    this.h = this.w;
144	  }
145	
146	  /** Gore.NewGore（Gore.cs:1306-1482）：池满/type 非法则弃；返回 null */
147	  static newGore(
148	    type: number, x: number, y: number, vx: number, vy: number, scale = 1, perfect = false,
149	  ): GorePiece | null {
150	    if (type < 0 || livePool.size >= MAX_GORE) return null;
151	    const g = new GorePiece(type, x, y, scale);
152	    g.vx = vx; g.vy = vy;
153	    if (!perfect) {
154	      // :1347-1348 随机扰动：vy -= Next(10,31)*0.1 ∈ [-3.0,-1.0]；vx += Next(-20,21)*0.1 ∈ [-2.0,2.0]
155	      g.vy -= irand2(10, 31) * 0.1;
156	      g.vx += irand2(-20, 21) * 0.1;
157	    }
158	    g.rotation = 0;
159	    g.alpha = 0;
160	    // :1361-1374 粘滞与时档
161	    if (NON_STICKY_TYPES.has(type)) {
162	      g.sticky = false;
163	    } else if (type >= 375 && type <= 377) {
164	      g.sticky = false;
165	      g.alpha = 100;
166	    } else {
167	      g.sticky = true;
168	      g.timeLeft = GORE_TIME;
169	    }
170	    // :1391-1398 多帧族（Frame=SpriteFrame(1,rows)，行 0 起；1218 首帧随机行在 Update 内）
171	    if (FRAME_ROWS[type]) g.row = 0;
172	    // :1423-1425 SpecialAI==1（帆降）：初速整体覆写为 ((rand-0.5)*3, rand*2π)——
173	    // vy 兼作相位累加器，Gore_UpdateSail 据此走圆弧下落
174	    if (SAIL_TYPES.has(type)) {
175	      g.vx = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 3;
176	      g.vy = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
177	    }
178	    // :1442-1449（907-909 随机起始帧；本表未出现，保留语义）
179	    if (type >= 907 && type <= 909) g.row = irand(3);
180	    livePool.add(g);
181	    return g;
182	  }
183	
184	  /** 每固定 tick（Gore.Update，Gore.cs:313-1102） */
185	  fixedUpdate(dt: number, game: GameHooks): void {
186	    void dt;
187	    if (this.dead) return;
188	    const st = game.world.store;
189	    const t = this.goreId;
190	
191	    // sticky 入口：越界消亡 + 速度钳 32（:319-329 / DeactivateIfOutsideOfWorld :297-311）
192	    if (this.sticky) {
193	      const tx = Math.floor(this.x / 16), ty = Math.floor(this.y / 16);
194	      if (!st.inBounds(tx, ty)) { this.kill(); return; }
195	      const sp = Math.hypot(this.vx, this.vy);
196	      if (sp > 32) { this.vx *= 32 / sp; this.vy *= 32 / sp; }
197	    }
198	
199	    // SpecialAI 7（1218）：UpdateLightningBunnySparks :244-262（switch :331-345 早退）
200	    if (t === 1218) {
201	      if (this.frameCounter === 0) {
202	        this.frameCounter = 1;
203	        this.row = irand(3); // Frame(1,3) 内随机行
204	      }
205	      this.timeLeft -= vanishSpeed(t);
206	      if (this.timeLeft <= 0) { this.kill(); return; }
207	      this.alpha = Math.round(255 - (255 * Math.max(0, this.timeLeft)) / 15);
208	      this.x += this.vx;
209	      this.y += this.vy;
210	      if (this.alpha >= 255) this.kill();
211	      return;
212	    }
213	
214	    if (SAIL_TYPES.has(t)) { this.updateSail(game); return; }
215	
216	    // ---- 位移前物理（:352-769 链）----
217	    if (isDampRibbon(t)) {
218	      // :352-371 彩带阻尼
219	      this.vx *= 0.98;
220	      this.vy *= 0.98;
221	      if (this.vy < this.scale) this.vy += 0.05;
222	      if (this.vy > 0.1) this.rotation += this.vx > 0 ? 0.01 : -0.01;
223	      this.vy += 0.2;                 // 默认重力支（:760-764）
224	      this.rotation += this.vx * 0.05;
225	    } else if (SHRINK_TYPES.has(t)) {
226	      // :535-556 血块收缩（16/17 为 0.01 档——本表未出现，语义保留）
227	      this.vy *= 0.98;
228	      this.vx *= 0.98;
229	      this.scale -= (t === 16 || t === 17) ? 0.01 : 0.007;
230	      if (this.scale < 0.1) { this.scale = 0.1; this.alpha = 255; }
231	      this.resizeBox();
232	    } else {
233	      // 默认支（:760-764）：重力 0.2 + 随 vx 翻滚
234	      this.vy += 0.2;
235	      this.rotation += this.vx * 0.05;
236	    }
237	
238	    // ---- 碰撞/时档尾段（:929-1042）----
239	    if (this.sticky) {
240	      moveAndCollide(this, game.world, this.vx, this.vy);
241	      if (this.vy === 0) {
242	        // 落地摩擦（:1015-1029）：|vx|<0.01 归零
243	        this.vx *= 0.97;
244	        if (this.vx > -0.01 && this.vx < 0.01) this.vx = 0;
245	      }
246	      if (this.timeLeft > 0) this.timeLeft -= vanishSpeed(t);
247	      else this.alpha += vanishAlpha(t);
248	    } else {
249	      // :1041 非粘滞：两倍速渐隐
250	      this.alpha += 2 * vanishAlpha(t);
251	      this.x += this.vx;
252	      this.y += this.vy;
253	    }
254	
255	    if (this.alpha >= 255) this.kill();
256	  }
257	
258	  /** Gore_UpdateSail（Gore.cs:1231-1291）：相位下落；碰撞转落地滑移支 */
259	  private updateSail(game: GameHooks): void {
260	    const t = this.goreId;
261	    if (this.vy < 0) {
262	      // 落地滑移支（:1233-1261）：只推 velocity.X，不推进 position
263	      let moveX = this.vx;
264	      const size = Math.floor(Math.min(this.texW, this.texH) * 0.9 * this.scale);
265	      const probe = { x: this.x, y: this.y, w: size, h: size, vx: moveX, vy: 0.6, onGround: false, hitWall: false, hitHead: false };
266	      moveAndCollide(probe, game.world, moveX, 0.6);
267	      moveX = probe.vx * 0.97;
268	      if (moveX > -0.01 && moveX < 0.01) moveX = 0;
269	      if (this.timeLeft > 0) this.timeLeft -= vanishSpeed(t);
270	      else this.alpha += vanishAlpha(t);
271	      this.vx = moveX;
272	      if (this.alpha >= 255) this.kill();
273	      return;
274	    }
275	    // 下落支（:1263-1290）：vy 兼作相位累加器；UnitY.RotatedBy(vy) = (−sin, cos)，Y 取 |·|
276	    this.vy += Math.PI / 60;
277	    const dx = -Math.sin(this.vy) * 2 * 2;
278	    const dy = Math.abs(Math.cos(this.vy)) * 3 * 2;
279	    const size = Math.floor(Math.min(this.texW, this.texH) * 0.9 * this.scale);
280	    const body = { x: this.x, y: this.y, w: size, h: size, vx: dx, vy: dy, onGround: false, hitWall: false, hitHead: false };
281	    moveAndCollide(body, game.world, dx, dy);
282	    if (body.vx !== dx || body.vy !== dy) this.vy = -1; // 碰撞 → 落地支
283	    this.x = body.x;
284	    this.y = body.y;
285	    this.rotation = Math.atan2(body.vy, body.vx) + Math.PI;
286	    if (this.timeLeft > 0) this.timeLeft -= vanishSpeed(t);
287	    else this.alpha += vanishAlpha(t);
288	    if (this.alpha >= 255) this.kill();
289	  }
290	
291	  private kill(): void {
292	    if (this.dead) return;
293	    this.dead = true;
294	    livePool.delete(this);
295	  }
296	
297	  /** Renderer 统一绘制（Main.DrawGore Main.cs:21712-21742：中心锚 + scale + rotation） */
298	  draw(): void { /* 见 Renderer.drawGorePieces */ }
299	
300	  /** 多帧行数（1 = 整张贴图） */
301	  get frameRows(): number {
302	    return FRAME_ROWS[this.goreId] ?? 1;
303	  }
304	}
305	
306	/** 表项 → 具体 gore id（区间/候选均匀取值；不可求值跳过） */
307	function entryId(e: GoreSpawnEntry): number | null {
308	  if (typeof e.id === 'number') return e.id;
309	  if (e.idMin != null && e.idMax != null) return e.idMin + irand(e.idMax - e.idMin + 1);
310	  if (e.ids?.length) return e.ids[irand(e.ids.length)];
311	  return null;
312	}
313	
314	/** 表项 → 出生点（基准 = NPC 左上角；编译字段线性组合，未编译回退原点） */
315	function entryPos(e: GoreSpawnEntry, s: GoreNpcSource): { x: number; y: number } {
316	  return {
317	    x: s.x + (e.ox ?? 0) + (e.fx ?? 0) * s.w + (e.rx ? Math.random() * s.w : 0),
318	    y: s.y + (e.oy ?? 0) + (e.fy ?? 0) * s.h + (e.ry ? Math.random() * s.h : 0),
319	  };
320	}
321	
322	/** 表项 → 初始速度。语义 = NewGore 的 Velocity 实参整体替换（不含 NewGore 自带的
323	 *  随机扰动）：常量 > 均匀随机 > NPC 速度×k > 击退方向 > 继承 NPC 速度（缺省） */
324	function axisVel(v: number | undefined, k: number | undefined, m: number | undefined,
325	  a: number | undefined, hd: boolean, inherit: number, hitDir: number): number {
326	  if (v != null) return v;
327	  if (a != null) return uniform(m ?? 0, a);
328	  if (k != null) return k * inherit;
329	  if (hd) return hitDir;
330	  return inherit;
331	}
332	function entryVel(e: GoreSpawnEntry, s: GoreNpcSource): { vx: number; vy: number } {
333	  const hd = s.hitDir ?? 0;
334	  return {
335	    vx: axisVel(e.vx, e.kx, e.mx, e.ax, !!e.hd, s.vx, hd),
336	    vy: axisVel(e.vy, e.ky, e.my, e.ay, false, s.vy, hd),
337	  };
338	}
339	
340	/** 表项 ai 门（原版 `ai[N] == V`）：不满足跳过 */
341	function aiGateOk(e: GoreSpawnEntry, s: GoreNpcSource): boolean {
342	  if (!e.ai?.length) return true;
343	  return e.ai.every((g) => {
344	    const v = g.slot === 0 ? s.ai0 : g.slot === 1 ? s.ai1 : g.slot === 2 ? s.ai2 : s.ai3;
345	    return g.not ? v !== g.value : v === g.value;
346	  });
347	}
348	
349	/** 按表炸出某 NPC 的全部死亡 gore；add = 实体入桶回调。返回生成数 */
350	export function spawnDeathGore(s: GoreNpcSource, add: (p: GorePiece) => void): number {
351	  if (s.vanillaId == null) return 0;
352	  const list = deathGoreFor(s.vanillaId);
353	  let n = 0;
354	  for (const e of list) {
355	    if (!aiGateOk(e, s)) continue;
356	    const times = e.loop ?? 1;
357	    for (let k = 0; k < times; k++) {
358	      const type = entryId(e);
359	      if (type == null || type < 0) continue; // expr 留档条目（提取器不可静态求值）
360	      const pos = entryPos(e, s);
361	      const vel = entryVel(e, s);
362	      const scale = e.scale ?? (e.scaleExpr === 'scale' ? s.vanillaScale : 1);
363	      const g = GorePiece.newGore(type, pos.x, pos.y, vel.vx, vel.vy, scale, !!e.perfect);
364	      if (g) { add(g); n++; }
365	    }
366	  }
367	  return n;
368	}
369	
370	/** Enemy/Critter 死亡钩子：查表炸 gore（props 桶；表空 = 原版只出尘，返回 0）。
371	 *  hitDir = HitEffect 的 hitDirection（击退方向，`new Vector2(hitDirection, 0f)` 类表项用） */
372	export function spawnNpcDeathGore(npc: GoreNpcSource, game: GameHooks, hitDir?: number): number {
373	  const add = (game as unknown as {
374	    entities?: { add?: (p: unknown, bucket: string) => void };
375	  }).entities;
376	  if (!add?.add) return 0;
377	  const src: GoreNpcSource = hitDir === undefined ? npc : { ...npc, hitDir };
378	  return spawnDeathGore(src, (p) => add.add!(p, 'props'));
379	}
380	

```

150	    expect(ticks).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(GORE_TIME);        // 先驻留 600 帧
151	    expect(ticks).toBeLessThan(GORE_TIME + 300);            // 再 ~255 帧渐隐
152	    expect(liveGorePieces().length).toBe(0);                // 池同步剔除
153	    resetGorePool();
154	  });
155	
156	  it('血块收缩族（11-13 等）：非粘滞，v*=0.98 + scale-=0.007 + alpha+=2/帧（:535-556/:1041）', () => {
157	    resetGorePool();
158	    const w = makeWorld();
159	    const game = makeGame(w);
160	    const p = GorePiece.newGore(11, 10 * TILE, 5 * TILE, 1, 1, 1)!;
161	    expect(p.sticky).toBe(false);                           // NewGore :1361
162	    const vy0 = p.vy, vx0 = p.vx;
163	    p.fixedUpdate(1, game);
164	    expect(p.vy).toBeCloseTo(vy0 * 0.98, 6);
165	    expect(p.vx).toBeCloseTo(vx0 * 0.98, 6);
166	    expect(p.scale).toBeCloseTo(1 - 0.007, 6);
167	    expect(p.alpha).toBe(2);                                // 2×DisappearSpeedAlpha
168	    // 收缩到底：scale 钳 0.1 + alpha=255 即刻消亡（:540-544）
169	    // alpha 先于 scale 收到底（255/2≈128 帧 vs 0.9/0.007≈129 帧）→ 消亡时 scale≈0.104
170	    let ticks = 1;
171	    while (!p.dead && ticks++ < 400) p.fixedUpdate(1, game);
172	    expect(p.dead).toBe(true);
173	    expect(p.scale).toBeLessThanOrEqual(0.104 + 1e-9);
174	    resetGorePool();
175	  });
176	
177	  it('帆降族（860 海盗船残骸，SpecialAI 1）：相位下落、碰撞转落地滑移（:1231-1291）', () => {
178	    resetGorePool();
179	    const w = makeWorld();
180	    const game = makeGame(w);
181	    const p = GorePiece.newGore(860, 10 * TILE, 5 * TILE, 0.5, 0, 1)!;
182	    expect(p.sticky).toBe(true);
183	    // SpecialAI 1 初速覆写（:1423-1425）：vx ∈ [-1.5,1.5]、vy ∈ [0,2π)
184	    expect(p.vx).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(-1.5);
185	    expect(p.vx).toBeLessThanOrEqual(1.5);
186	    expect(p.vy).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0);
187	    expect(p.vy).toBeLessThan(Math.PI * 2);
188	    p.vy = 0.5;                                            // 固定相位走下落支
189	    const y0 = p.y;
190	    p.fixedUpdate(1, game);
191	    expect(p.y).toBeGreaterThan(y0);                        // 下落支位移
192	    expect(p.vy).toBeGreaterThan(0);                        // 相位累加 MathF.PI/60
193	    // 拖到落地（碰撞 → vy=-1 转滑移支；滑移支不再推进 position）
194	    let ticks = 0;
195	    while (p.vy >= 0 && ticks++ < 4000) p.fixedUpdate(1, game);
196	    expect(p.vy).toBe(-1);                                  // 碰撞转支（:1277-1279）
197	    const yLand = p.y;
198	    p.fixedUpdate(1, game);
199	    expect(p.y).toBe(yLand);                                // 落地支只推 velocity.X
200	    resetGorePool();
201	  });
202	
203	  it('NewGore 随机扰动（:1347-1348）：vy∈[-3,-1]、vx∈[-2,+2]；perfect 不扰动', () => {
204	    resetGorePool();
205	    for (let i = 0; i < 200; i++) {
206	      const p = GorePiece.newGore(4, 0, 0, 0, 0, 1)!;
207	      expect(p.vy).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(-3.0);
208	      expect(p.vy).toBeLessThanOrEqual(-1.0);
209	      expect(p.vx).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(-2.0);
210	      expect(p.vx).toBeLessThanOrEqual(2.0);
211	    }
212	    const q = GorePiece.newGore(4, 5, 6, 1.5, -2.5, 1, true)!;
213	    expect(q.vx).toBe(1.5);
214	    expect(q.vy).toBe(-2.5);
215	    resetGorePool();
216	  });
217	
218	  it('600 槽池满即弃（Gore.cs:1328-1340），resetGorePool 后恢复', () => {
219	    resetGorePool();

221	    for (let i = 0; i < 700; i++) {

227	    expect(GorePiece.newGore(4, 0, 0, 0, 0, 1)).not.toBeNull();
228	    resetGorePool();
229	  });

237	    expect(p.dead).toBe(true);

239	  });
240	});

242	describe('③ 表驱动死亡钩', () => {
243	  it('僵尸死亡炸 5 块（头/臂×2/腿×2），出生点带 +20/+34 纵偏移', () => {
244	    resetGorePool();

247	    expect(n).toBe(5);

249	    expect(got[0].x).toBe(100);
250	    expect(got[0].y).toBe(200);

253	    resetGorePool();

256	  it('史莱姆（1/16）无表项不出块；庆典史莱姆 ai[1]==1345 出彩带 276-282', () => {
257	    resetGorePool();
258	    const got: GorePiece[] = [];
259	    expect(spawnDeathGore(src({ vanillaId: 16 }), (p) => got.push(p))).toBe(0);
260	    expect(spawnDeathGore(src({ vanillaId: 1 }), (p) => got.push(p))).toBe(0);
261	    expect(spawnDeathGore(src({ vanillaId: 1, ai1: 999 }), (p) => got.push(p))).toBe(0);
262	    const ribbons: GorePiece[] = [];

264	    expect(ribbons[0].goreId).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(276);
265	    expect(ribbons[0].goreId).toBeLessThanOrEqual(282);
266	    // 表速 new Vector2(0f, -10f)，叠加 NewGore 扰动（:1347-1348）后：
267	    // vx ∈ [-2,2]、vy ∈ [-13,-11]
268	    expect(ribbons[0].vx).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(-2);
269	    expect(ribbons[0].vx).toBeLessThanOrEqual(2);

275	  it('EoC（4）：for(<2) 循环体 4 调用 = 8 块（loop 字段），速度为均匀随机 ±6', () => {
276	    for (let i = 0; i < 50; i++) {
277	      resetGorePool();                                    // 600 槽池：循环内复位防满池截断

export const ENEMY_MONEY_DROP_MULTIPLIER: readonly CurveKey[] = [
  { input: GDL.CLASSIC, output: 1 }, { input: GDL.EXPERT, output: 2.5 },
  { input: GDL.MASTER, output: 2.5 }, { input: GDL.LEGENDARY, output: 3.5 },
];

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Main.Difficulty / expertMode / masterMode（Main.cs:2760-2786）
//   ★ GameMode 3（旅程）**不**抬档——旅程世界的 NPC 数值=经典档；真正的旅程
//     0.5× 由 _gameModeDifficultyOverride（Main.cs:17245，旅程"给 NPC 的强度"
//     滑杆 StrengthMultiplierToGiveNPCs）注入，本仓未建模旅程能力滑杆 → 恒 null。
//   ★ getGoodWorld（for the worthy 种子）整体 +1 档（:2783-2785）——经典 FTW 世界
//     的 expertMode 即为 true，克脑 40 爬行者/饥饿者专家分支/持械僵尸全数生效。
//   我方 world.difficulty：0 经典 / 1 专家 / 2 大师 / 3 旅程（World.ts:73）。
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
export function mainDifficulty(world: Pick<World, 'difficulty' | 'seedFlags'> | null | undefined): number {
  let num: number = GDL.CLASSIC;
  if (world) {
    if (world.difficulty === 1) num = GDL.EXPERT;
    else if (world.difficulty === 2) num = GDL.MASTER;
    if (world.seedFlags?.getGoodWorld) num += 1;
  }
  return num;
}

/** Main.expertMode（Main.cs:2762）：Difficulty >= Expert（getGoodWorld 抬档后同样生效） */
export function mainExpertMode(world: Pick<World, 'difficulty' | 'seedFlags'> | null | undefined): boolean {
  return mainDifficulty(world) >= GDL.EXPERT;
}

/** Main.masterMode（Main.cs:2760）：Difficulty >= Master */
export function mainMasterMode(world: Pick<World, 'difficulty' | 'seedFlags'> | null | undefined): boolean {
  return mainDifficulty(world) >= GDL.MASTER;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// NPCID.Sets（NPCID.cs：Factory.CreateBoolSet(...) 等价"集合包含"）
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

export const NEEDS_EXPERT_SCALING = new Set([25, 30, 665, 33, 112, 666, 261, 265, 371, 516, 519, 397, 396, 398, 491]);
/** ProjectileNPC（:4801）：弹体 NPC——不缩 life/value，ExpertHardmode 段只乘 damage */
export const PROJECTILE_NPC = new Set([25, 30, 665, 33, 112, 666, 261, 265, 371, 516, 519]);

	public static int NewGore(Vector2 Position, Vector2 Velocity, int Type, float Scale = 1f)
	{
		if (Main.netMode == 2)
		{
			return 600;
		}
		if (Main.gamePaused)
		{
			return 600;
		}
		if (WorldGen.isGeneratingOrLoadingWorld)
		{
			return 600;
		}
		if (Main.rand == null)
		{
			Main.rand = new UnifiedRandom();
		}
		if (Type == -1)
		{
			return 600;
		}
		int num = 600;
		for (int i = 0; i < 600; i++)
		{
			if (!Main.gore[i].active)

				num = i;
				break;
			}
		}
		if (num == 600)
		{
			return num;
		}
		Main.gore[num].Frame = new SpriteFrame(1, 1);
		Main.gore[num].frameCounter = 0;
		Main.gore[num].behindTiles = false;
		Main.gore[num].light = 0f;
		Main.gore[num].position = Position;
		Main.gore[num].velocity = Velocity;
		Main.gore[num].velocity.Y -= (float)Main.rand.Next(10, 31) * 0.1f;
		Main.gore[num].velocity.X += (float)Main.rand.Next(-20, 21) * 0.1f;
		Main.gore[num].type = Type;
		Main.gore[num].active = true;
		Main.gore[num].alpha = 0;

		{

			Main.gore[num].type = Type;

## 🤖 Assistant · 2026-08-13T08:39:33.395Z · glm-x-preview-260804

<details open>
<summary>💭 thinking</summary>

````
The 1456 decompile genuinely shows `return new Color(255, 255, 255, 0);` for gore 16/17. This differs from 1405 (`r,g,b` from lighting color). 

Per project rules: "1456 版数值/行为一律取最终态;1405 与 1456 冲突时以 1456 为准并在注释标明两版行号" — so 1456 wins: `Color(255,255,255,0)`.

But what does Color(255,255,255,0) mean for rendering? A=0 → in XNA alpha blending that would be invisible... but hold on! XNA SpriteBatch default blend is AlphaBlend where ColorSourceBlend = Blend.SourceAlpha. So A=0 → invisible.

Hmm, but wait — maybe not! Let me reconsider: maybe in FNA/XNA the SpriteBatch uses `BlendState.AlphaBlend` = `{ ColorSourceBlend = Blend.One, ... }`?? Let me recall XNA's BlendState.AlphaBlend definition:

```csharp
public static readonly BlendState AlphaBlend = new BlendState {
    Name = "BlendState.AlphaBlend",
    ColorSourceBlend = Blend.One,
    AlphaSourceBlend = Blend.One,
    ColorDestinationBlend = Blend.InverseSourceAlpha,
    AlphaDestinationBlend = Blend.InverseSourceAlpha,
};
```

YES! That's it! XNA's built-in `BlendState.AlphaBlend` uses **SourceBlend = One, DestinationBlend = InverseSourceAlpha** — this is the **PREMULTIPLIED alpha** blend mode! (XNA's SpriteBatch default blend is BlendState.AlphaBlend which expects premultiplied alpha content; XNA's content pipeline premultiplies textures by default.)

So: final.rgb = src.rgb * 1 + dst.rgb * (1 - src.a). With color = Color(255,255,255,0): src.rgb = texture.rgb * 1.0 (white tint, rgb unaffected by A since SourceBlend=One), src.a = texA * 0 = 0 → destination factor = 1 → dst.rgb + src.rgb → ADDITIVE appearance! 

So `new Color(255,255,255,0)` renders the fire texture ADDITIVELY (glowing fire, no darkening). That's exactly why fire gores glow — and it confirms the "自发光" (self-illuminating) semantic: the fire puff is drawn additively full-bright plus emits light via Lighting.AddLight.

So the semantics to implement for gore 16/17:
1. **Spawn (NewGore :1487-1490)**: alpha=100, scale=0.7, light=1.0.
2. **Update (:546-556)**: v*=0.98 both axes, scale -= 0.01/t (0.01 档), clamp 0.1 → alpha=255 (dies).
3. **Light emission (Update tail :1078-1101)**: if light > 0: base = light*scale on each channel; type 16 → B*=0.3, G*=0.8; type 17 → G*=0.6, R*=0.3; AddLight at gore center tile ((pos + texSize*scale/2)/16).
4. **Render (GetAlpha :1487-1490)**: additive full-bright white (A=0 with premultiplied blend = additive) — i.e., ignore local lighting for RGB and draw additively.

And 1405 cross-ref: `r/g/b = newColor (lighting)` with standard alpha — I'll note both line numbers in a comment per project convention.

  private drawGorePiece(ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, p: GorePiece, z: number) {
    const img = this.atlas!.ensureVImage(`vanilla/Gore_${p.goreId}.png`);
    if (!img || !img.complete || img.naturalWidth === 0) return;
    const rows = p.frameRows;
    const fh = img.naturalHeight / rows;
    const sh = Math.floor(fh);
    const sw = img.naturalWidth;
    // Gore.GetAlpha（:1486）：opacity = (255 - alpha)/255
    ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, (255 - p.alpha) / 255);
    if (p.rotation === 0 && p.scale === 1) {
      // 直绘路径（Main.cs:21706-21742 原点即贴图中心：落点 + 尺寸/2）
      ctx.drawImage(img, 0, p.row * sh, sw, sh, p.x, p.y, sw, sh);
      return;
    }
    ctx.save();
    ctx.translate(p.x + (sw * p.scale) / 2, p.y + (sh * p.scale) / 2);
    if (p.rotation) ctx.rotate(p.rotation);
    if (p.scale !== 1) ctx.scale(p.scale, p.scale);
    ctx.drawImage(img, 0, p.row * sh, sw, sh, -sw / 2, -sh / 2, sw, sh);
    ctx.restore();
    void z;
  }

Where X resolves through alias→index var→entry list.

Data structures:
```js

On reassignment `num569 = Gore.NewGore(...)`: goreIdx.set(name, newEntries) — handleGoreCall must return the emitted entries so the caller can bind. Currently handleGoreCall emits internally. I'll have handleGoreCall return the array of emitted entry objects (and bind via a module-level `lastEmit` mechanism + the LHS detection in the main loop).

Ordering: the main loop processes a logical line; the NewGore may be preceded by the LHS declaration in the same logical line (`int num569 = Gore.NewGore(...)` is ONE logical line). So in the main loop, for a segment containing `Gore.NewGore`, first extract the LHS name from the segment: `/^(?:int|Gore)\s+(\w+)\s*=\s*Gore\.NewGore/` or `/^(\w+)\s*=\s*Gore\.NewGore/` at the segment start. Then call handleGoreCall and bind.

Alias lines `Gore gore2 = Main.gore[num569];` appear on their OWN logical line (or same line? `int num329 = Gore.NewGore(position, new Vector2(0f, -10f), num328); Gore gore2 = Main.gore[num329]; gore2.scale *= ...` — from the source, they're on separate physical lines but could merge into one logical line if parens don't balance... they do balance per statement, so logical lines are per-statement ✓). I'll implement a `handlePostLine(plain, lineNo)` that parses the whole line for all such statements (a line could contain several, e.g. multiple `.velocity.X += 1f;` — no, one per line in the source).

Multiple statements per logical line: possible for `Gore gore2 = Main.gore[num329]; gore2.scale *= ...` on the same logical line if... they're separate physical lines with balanced parens → separate logical lines ✓.

Now the runtime (GorePiece.ts):

```ts
export interface GoreSpawnEntry {
  ...
  idAlt?: number;                 // IsShimmerVariant ? idAlt : id
  gates?: GoreGate[];
  jx?: [number, number]; jy?: ...

  post?: PostOp[];
}
```

Gate evaluation:
```ts
function gateOk(e, s): boolean {
  for (const g of e.gates ?? []) {
    switch (g.k) {
      case 'alt': if (!cmp(s.altTexture ?? 0, g.op, g.v)) return false; break;
      case 'sdir': if (!cmp(s.spriteDirection ?? 1, g.op, g.v)) return false; break;
      case 'shimmer': if ((!!s.isShimmerVariant) === g.not... 
      case 'dmg': cmp(s.dmg ?? 9999, g.op, g.v)
      case 'net': cmp(GORE_NET_MODE, g.op, g.v)
      case 'rnd': cmp(irand(g.n), g.op, g.v)
      case 'redhat': redHatOk(s)
    }
  }
}
```

Note: `rnd` gates with op '==' and v=0: `irand(n) === 0` → probability 1/n ✓. `!= 0` → 1-1/n ✓.

redHat (NPC.cs:67282): type 35→ai3==1; 36/32→lai3==1; 33→ai3==1; else false.

Pos computation extended:
```
x = s.x + ox + fx*w + (rx? rand*w:0) + rwx jitter + jx jitter + dx*direction + rot terms + dc
```

Vel computation: existing + dc/de/rv.

Post application: for op in post: apply to the GorePiece after newGore.

- 16/17: alpha=100, scale=0.7, light=1.

Also the scale shrink for 16/17 is 0.01 — already handled in fixedUpdate (SHRINK branch checks t===16||17 → 0.01 ✓ but 16/17 are NOT in SHRINK_TYPES; they're handled in the separate `else if (type === 16 || type === 17)` — wait, the current code has:

```ts

Hmm, the code has the 16/17 branch INSIDE the SHRINK_TYPES branch — but 16/17 aren't in SHRINK_TYPES, so they fall to the default branch (gravity + rotation). In vanilla, 16/17 have their OWN branch (`else if (type == 16 || type == 17)`) which is equivalent to the shrink branch but with 0.01. So the current code is buggy for 16/17 (they'd get gravity+rotation instead of shrink). I should add 16/17 to SHRINK_TYPES (or a separate set) so they hit the shrink path with 0.01. Since SHRINK_TYPES is also used... only in that one place. I'll add a separate check.

Also the GetAlpha for 16/17 (additive full-bright) → the renderer. `drawGorePiece` uses `ctx.globalAlpha = (255-alpha)/255`. For 16/17 I need: additive blending (globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter') and full-bright (ignore scene lighting — the current renderer doesn't apply lighting to gore at all, so "full-bright" is automatic). And A=0 with premultiplied blend = additive → in canvas terms: `globalCompositeOperation='lighter'` with globalAlpha... hmm. Careful: the premultiplied additive semantics mean src.rgb contributes fully (not scaled by alpha). Canvas 'lighter' adds src*alpha to dst. With globalAlpha=1 (or (255-alpha)/255) — the texture's own alpha channel still gates (fire puff has soft alpha edges). In XNA premultiplied additive: src.rgb is premultiplied by texA already (content pipeline), and SourceBlend=One → adds texRGB*texA. Canvas 'lighter' with globalAlpha=g adds texRGBA*g → rgb added = texRGB*texA*g ✓ same. So: `ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter'; ctx.globalAlpha = (255-alpha)/255` gives the same result as XNA's Color(255,255,255,0) premultiplied draw (rgb added, alpha channel of target also increases — minor difference, invisible in practice).

So for 16/17: draw with 'lighter'. That's the "自发光" visual. Plus light emission.

	public static Vector2 NextVector2Circular(this UnifiedRandom r, float circleHalfWidth, float circleHalfHeight)
	{
		return r.NextVector2Unit() * new Vector2(circleHalfWidth, circleHalfHeight) * r.NextFloat();
	}

	public static Vector2 NextVector2CircularEdge(this UnifiedRandom r, float circleHalfWidth, float circleHalfHeight)
	{
		return r.NextVector2Unit() * new Vector2(circleHalfWidth, circleHalfHeight);
	}

	public static Vector2D NextVector2DSquare(this UnifiedRandom r, double min, double max)
	{
		//IL_0018: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
		return new Vector2D((max - min) * r.NextDouble() + min, (max - min) * r.NextDouble() + min);
	public static Vector2 RandomVector2(UnifiedRandom random, float min, float max)
	{
		return new Vector2((max - min) * (float)random.NextDouble() + min, (max - min) * (float)random.NextDouble() + min);
	}

	public static Vector2D RandomVector2D(UnifiedRandom random, double min, double max)
	{
		//IL_0018: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)

{ w: b.readUInt32BE(16), h: b.readUInt32BE(20) };\n    }\n  } catch { /* 缺贴图 → null，运行时回退 32px 盒（Gore.cs:999） */ }\n  pngCache.set(id, out);\n  return out;\n}\n\n// ============ 工具 ============\n/** 括号深度（>0 = 未闭合） */\nfunction parenDepth(s) {\n  let d = 0;\n  for (const c of s) { if (c === '(') d++; else if (c === ')') d--; }\n  return d;\n}\n\n/** s[i] 须为 '('：取匹配内文 */\nfunction parenBody(s, i) {\n  let d = 0;\n  for (let j = i; j < s.length; j++) {\n    if (s[j] === '(') d++;\n    else if (s[j] === ')') { d--; if (d === 0) return s.slice(i + 1, j); }\n  }\n  return s.slice(i + 1);\n}\n\n/** || c === ']') d--;\n    if (c === ',' && d === 0) { out.push(cur.trim()); cur = ''; continue; }\n    cur += c;\n  }\n  if (cur.trim()) out.push(cur.trim());\n  return out;\n}\n\n/** 顶层 `sep` out.push(cur.trim());\n  return out;\n}\n\n/** 顶层 ± 项切分 → [{t, sign}]（一元负号并入首项/括号项） */\nfunction splitTerms(s) {\n  const out = [];\n  let d = 0, cur = '', sign = 1;\n  for (let i = 0; i < s.length; NewGore(Vector2 Position, Vector2 Velocity, int Type, float Scale=1f) 命名实参归位 */\nfunction namedArgs(args) {\n  const map = { Position: args[0], Velocity: args[1], Type: args[2], Scale: args[3] };\n  for (const a of args) {\n    const m = a.match(/^(Position|Velocity|Type|Scale)\\s*:\\s*(.+)$/s);\n    if (m) map[m[1]] = m[2].trim();\n  }\n  return map;\n}\n\n/** 数字后缀剥离（f/F/d/D/m/M）+ 守卫条件解析 ============\n/** type 谓词全集（同条件内 || 并集；range 先消耗原文再扫单边界/!=） */\nfunction parseTypeCond(cond) {\n  const res = { include: new Set(), hasInclude: false, exclude: new Set(), lt: null, lte: null, gates ============\n/** NPCID.Sets.IsTownPet（NPCID.cs:4444）——静态集合，OR 项直接展开进 include */\nconst IS_TOWN_PET = new Set([637, 638, 656, 670, 678, 679, 680, 681, 682, 683, 684]);\n\n/** 单个子句归类 → {gate?, typePred: true };\n  if (t === 'NPCID.Sets.IsTownPet[type]') return { staticTypes: new Set(IS_TOWN_PET) };\n  if ((m = t.match(/^!IsShimmerVariant$/))) return { gate: { k: 'shim', not: true } };\n  if ((m = t.match(/^IsShimmerVariant$/))) return { gate: { k: 'shim', not: false } };\n  if ((m = t.match(/^altTexture\\s*(==|!=|>=|<=|>|<)\\s*(-?\\d+)$/))) return { gate: { k: 'alt', op: m[1], v: +m[2] } t.match(/^dmg\\s*(==|!=|>=|<=|>|<)\\s*(-?[\\d.]+)[fFdD]?$/))) return { gate: { k: 'dmg', op: m[1], v: +m[2] } };\n  if ((m = t.match(/^Main\\.netMode\\s*(==|!=)\\s*(\\d+)$/))) return { gate: { k: 'net', op: m[1], v: +m[2] } };\n  if ((m = t.match(/^Main\\.rand\\.Next\\((\\d+)\\)\\s*(==|!=)\\s*(\\d+)$/))) {\n    return { gate: { k: 'rnd', n: +m[1], op: m[2], v: +m[3] } };\n  }\n  if (/^RedHatSkeletronAdjustmentsEnabled\\(\\)$/.test(t)) return { gate: { k: 'redhat' } };\n  if (/UsesPartyHat|GetPartyHatColor|townNPC/.test(t)) return { party: true };\n  if ((m = t.match(/^([A-Za-z_]\\w*)\\s*(<=|<)\\s*(\\d+)$/)) && loopNames.has(m[1])) {\n    return { loopGate: { var: m[1], op: m[2], v: +m[3] } };\n  }\n  return null;\n}\n\n/** 剥一层包裹括号（子句整体被括号包住时） */\nfunction unwrap(t) {\n  let s = t.trim();\n  while (s.startsWith('(') && parenBody(s, 0).length === s.length - 2) s = parenBody(s, 0).trim();\n  return s;\n}\n\n/**\n * 守卫条件 → { gates, freeTypes, staticTypes, loopGates, party, unknown }\n *   单组（纯 &&）：gates = 全部门子句；多组（顶层 ||）：OR 内带 type 谓词的组只贡献\n *   freeTypes（该型免门），纯门组贡献 gates（见 _meta.notes 的 OR 语义说明）。\n */\nfunction parseGateClauses(cond, loopNames) {\n  const out = { gates: [], freeTypes: new Set(), staticTypes: new Set(), loopGates: [], party: false, unknown: [] };\n  if (!cond || cond === 'else') return out;\n  const groups = splitTop(cond, '|');\n  const multi = groups.length > 1;\n  for (const g of groups) {\n    const clauses = splitTop(g, '&').map(unwrap);\n    const gates = [], typesInGroup = new Set();\n    for (const cl of clauses) {\n      // 组内可能仍是嵌套 OR（`(type == 186 || type == 432)`）→ 递归判 type 谓词\n      if (/^type\\s*(==|>=|<=|>|<|!=)/.test(cl) || /^type\\s*==/.test(cl)) { typesInGroup.add(0); continue; }\n      if (/^NPCID\\.Sets\\.IsTownPet\\[type\\]$/.test(cl)) { for (const t of IS_TOWN_PET) out.staticTypes.add(t); continue; }\n      const c = classifyClause(cl, loopNames);\n      if (!c) { out;\n}\n\n// ============ gore id 表达式求值 ============\nfunction evalIdExpr(expr) {\n  const s = String(expr).trim();\n  let m;\n  if ((m = s.match(/^(-?\\d+)[fFdD]?$/))) return { kind: 'id', id: +m[1] };\n  if ((m = s.match(/^(\\w+)\\s*\\?\\s*(-?\\d+)\\s*:\\s*(-?\\d+)$/))) {\n    // IsShimmerVariant ? 微光变体 : 常规 —— 常规态取 else 支，变体 id 进 idAlt\n    return { kind: 'id', id: +m[3], idAlt: +m[2] };\n  }\n  if ((m = s.match(/^(-?\\d+)\\s*\\+\\s*Main\\.rand\\.Next\\(\\s*(\\d+)\\s*\\)$/))) {\n    const a = +m[1]; return { kind: 'range', min: a, max: a + +m[2] - 1 };\n  }\n  if ((m = };\n  }\n  // 局部变量 ± 常数偏移（人形族 `num46 + 3` 头/臂/腿族）\n  if ((m = s.match(/^([A-Za-z_]\\w*)\\s*\\+\\s*(-?\\d+)$/))) return { kind: 'local', name: m[1], off: +m[2] };\n  if ((m = null;\n}\n\n// ============ 主流程 ============\nconst src = readFileSync(NPC_CS, 'utf8').split('\\n');\nconst headIdx = src.findIndex((l) => l.includes('public void HitEffect(int hitDirection'));\nif []).filter((h) => h.line <= line);\n  return hist.length ? hist[hist.length - 1] : null;\n}\n\n// ---- 简易算术求值（局部变量/`type` 代数 id 还原用）----\n/** 递归下降：数 / 一元± / * / + -；括号。`type==N` 归一后为 0/1。返回 number | null */\nfunction evalArith(expr) {\n  const toks = [];\n  let s = String(expr).trim();\n  let m;\n  while (s.length) {\n    if (/^\\s/.test(s)) { s = s.slice(1); continue; }\n    if ((m = s.match(/^(-?\\d+\\.?\\d*)/))) { toks.push({ t: 'n', v: +m[1] }); s = s.slice(m[1].length); continue; }\n    if ((m = s.match(/^([-+*()/<>?:])/))) {\n      const op = m[1];\n      // 双字符 <= >= == != 跳过（归一阶段已替换成数字）\n      if ((op === '<' || op === '>') && '=<>=!'.includes(s[1] ?? '')) return null;\n      toks.push({ t: op }); s = s.slice(1); continue;\n    }\n    if ((m = s.match(/^==/))) return null;\n    return null;\n  }\n  let pos = 0;\n  const peek = () => toks[pos];\n  const eat = (t) => { if (toks[pos] && toks[pos].t === t) { pos++; return true; } return false; };\n  function ternary() {\n    const c = additive();\n    if (c == null) return null;\n    if (eat('?')) {\n      const a = ternary();\n      if (a == null || !eat(':')) return null;\n      const b = ternary();\n      if (b == null) return null;\n      return c !== 0 ? a : b;\n    }\n    return c;\n  }\n  function additive() {\n    let v = multiplicative();\n    while (v != null && (peek() && (peek().t === '+' || peek().t === '-'))) {\n      const op = peek().t; pos++;\n      const r = multiplicative();\n      if (r == null) return null;\n      v = op === '+' ? v + r : v - r;\n    }\n    return v;\n  }\n  function multiplicative() {\n    let v = unary();\n    while (v != null && peek() && peek().t === '*') {\n      pos++;\n      const r = unary();\n      if (r == null) return null;\n      v = v * r;\n    }\n    return v;\n  }\n  function unary() {\n    if (eat('-')) { const v = unary(); return v == null ? null : -v; }\n    if (eat('+')) return unary();\n    if (eat('(')) { const v = ternary(); return eat(')') ? v : null; }\n    const t = peek();\n    if (t && t.t === 'n') { pos++; return t.v; }\n    return null;\n  }\n  const out = ternary();\n  return pos === toks.length ? out : null;\n}\n\n/** `type` → 具体值代入 + `X == Y` 归一为 0/1（仅支持全数字比较） */\nfunction normalizeForArith(expr, t) {\n  let s = String(expr).replace(/\\btype\\b/g, String(t));\n  s = s.replace(/\\(\\s*(\\d+)\\s*==\\s*(\\d+)\\s*\\)/g, (_, a, b2) => (a === b2 ? '(1)' : '(0)'));\n  s = s.replace(/(\\d+)\\s*==\\s*(\\d+)/g, (_, a, b2) => (a === b2 ? '1' : '0'));\n  s = s.replace(/\\(\\s*\\)/g, '');\n  return s;\n}\n\n/** 单 type 值下的 id 求值 → {kind:id|range|ids} | null */\nfunction evalIdForType(expr, t, callLine, typeCtxId) {\n  const e = evalIdExpr(expr);\n  if (e) {\n    if (e.kind === 'id' || e.kind === 'range' || e.kind === 'ids') return e;\n    if (e.kind === 'local') {\n      const base = localValueForType(e.name, t, callLine, typeCtxId);\n      if (base == null) return null;\n      return applyOff(base, e.off);\n    }\n    return null;\n  }\n  // `X + local` / `local + rand(N)` / `local ± K` 反向形态\n  let m;\n  if ((m = expr.match(/^(-?\\d+)\\s*\\+\\s*([A-Za-z_]\\w*)$/))) {\n    const base = localValueForType(m[2], t, callLine, typeCtxId);\n    return base == null ? null : applyOff(base, +m[1]);\n  }\n  if ((m = expr.match(/^([A-Za-z_]\\w*)\\s*\\+\\s*Main\\.rand\\.Next\\(\\s*(\\d+)\\s*\\)$/))) {\n    const base = localValueForType(m[1], t, callLine, typeCtxId);\n    if (base == null || base.kind !== 'id') return null;\n    return { kind: 'range', min: base.id, max: base.id + +m[2] - 1 };\n  }\n  if ((m = expr.match(/^(-?\\d+)\\s*\\+\\s*\\(\\(Main\\.rand\\.Next\\(\\d+\\)\\s*==\\s*0\\)\\s*\\?\\s*(-?\\d+)\\s*:\\s*(-?\\d+)\\)$/))) {\n    return { kind: 'ids', ids: [+m[1] + +m[2], +m[1] + +m[3]] };\n  }\n  // 含 type 的代数式：代入后算术求值\n  if (/\\btype\\b/.test(expr)) {\n    const v = evalArith(normalizeForArith(expr, t));\n    if (v != null) return { kind: 'id', id: v };\n  }\n  return null;\n}\n\n/** 局部变量在 type=t 下的值（赋值流末值；声明含 type/循环变量则代数求值） */\nfunction localValueForType(name, t, callLine, typeCtxId) {\n  const hist = (locals.get(name) ?? []).filter((h) => h.line < callLine && (h.typeCtxId === typeCtxId || h.typeCtxId == null));\n  if (!hist.length) return null;\n  const last = hist[hist.length - 1];\n  if (last.loopRange) return { kind: 'range', min: last.loopRange[0], max: last.loopRange[1] };\n  const e = evalIdExpr(last.valExpr);\n  if (e && e.kind !== 'local') return e;\n  if (/\\btype\\b/.test(last.valExpr)) {\n    const v = evalArith(normalizeForArith(last.valExpr, t));\n    if (v != null) return { kind: 'id', id: v };\n  }\n  if (/^([A-Za-z_]\\w*)$/.test(last.valExpr.trim())) {\n    return localValueForType(last.valExpr.trim(), t, callLine, typeCtxId);\n  }\n  // 算术组合（如 `11 + num744`，num744 为循环变量）\n  let m;\n  if ((m = last.valExpr.match(/^(-?\\d+)\\s*\\+\\s*([A-Za-z_]\\w*)$/))) {\n    const base = localValueForType(m[2], t, callLine, typeCtxId);\n    return base == null ? null : applyOff(base, +m[1]);\n  }\n  return null;\n}\n\nfunction applyOff(v, off) {\n  if (v.kind === 'id') return { kind: 'id', id: v.id + off };\n  if (v.kind === 'range') return { kind: 'range', min: v.min + off, max: v.max + off };\n  if (v.kind === 'ids') return { kind: 'ids', ids: v.ids.map((x) => x + off) };\n  return null;\n}\n\n/** 逐 type 求值 → {fields} 统一 / {perType:Map} 分歧 / null 不可求值 */\nfunction resolveIdPerType(expr, target, callLine, typeCtxId) {\n  const per = new Map();\n  for (const t of target) {\n    const v = evalIdForType(expr, t, callLine, typeCtxId);\n    if (!v) return null;\n    per.set(t, v);\n  }\n  const uniq = [...new Set([...per.values()].map((v) => JSON.stringify(v)))];\n  if (uniq.length === 1) return { fields: idFields(JSON.parse(uniq[0])) };\n  const perMap = new Map();\n  for (const [t, v] of per) perMap.set(t, idFields(v));\n  return { perType: perMap };\n}\n\nfunction idFields(e) {\n  const f = {};\n  if (e.kind === 'id') {\n    f.id = e.id;\n    if (e.idAlt != null) f.idAlt = e.idAlt;   // IsShimmerVariant ? 加法（同键相加；区间端点相加） */\nfunction addIR(a, b) {\n  const out = { ...a };\n  for (const k of ['ox', 'oy', 'fx', 'fy', 'dx', 'dy']) {\n    if (b[k] != null) out[k] = +(((out[k] ?? 0) + b[k])).toFixed(4);\n []), ...b.rot];\n  return out;\n}\n\n/** IR 数值缩放（局部向量 × 系数） */\nfunction scaleIR(ir, k) {\n  const out = {};\n  for (const key of ['ox', 'oy', 'fx', 'fy', 'dx', 'dy']) {\n    if (ir[key] != null) out[key] = +(ir[key] * k).toFixed(4);\n  }\n  for (const key of ['rwx', 'rwy', 'jx', 'jy']) {\n    if (ir[key]) out[key] = [+(ir[key][0] * k).toFixed(4), +(ir[key][1] * k).toFixed(4)];\n  }\n  if (ir.dc) out.dc = [+(ir.dc[0] * Math.abs(k)).toFixed(4), +(ir.dc[1] * Math.abs(k)).toFixed(4)];\n  if (ir.rot) out.rot = ir.rot.map((r) => ({ ...r, k: +(r.k * k).toFixed(4) }));\n  return out;\n}\n\n/** Vector2 局部在 callLine 前的最新 IR（kind 须匹配） */\nfunction localIR(name, kind, callLine, seen = new Set()) {\n  if (seen.has(name)) return null;\n  seen.add(name);\n  const rec = vecIRFor(name, callLine);\n  if (!rec || rec.kind !== kind) return null;\n  if (rec.ir.__ref) return localIR(rec.ir.__ref, kind, callLine, seen);\n  return rec.ir;\n}\n\n/** 单项 → IR（pos 语义）| null */\nfunction posTermIR(term, callLine, seen) {\n  const t = term.trim();\n  let m;\n  if (t === 'position') return {};\n  if (t === 'base.Center') return { fx: 0.5, fy: 0.5 };\n  if (t === 'base.Top') return { fx: 0.5 };\n  if (t === 'base.Bottom') return { fx: 0.5, fy: 1 };\n  if (t.startsWith('new Vector2(')) {\n    const comps = splitArgs(parenBody(t, t.indexOf('(')));\n    if (comps.length === 1) {\n      const v = numLit(comps[0]);\n      return v == null ?
