     return true;\n      }\n      // `0f - (3f + Next(4)*0.3f)` 之类复合 → 项式代数\n      const ax = axisExpr(t, axis === 'x' ? 'width' : 'height');\n      if (!ax || ax.f != null || ax.r != null) return false;\n      if (ax.off != null) { out[`v${axis}`] = ax.off; return true; }\n      return false;\n    };\n    if (!comp(comps[0], 'x') || !comp(comps[1], 'y')) return null;\n    return out;\n  }\n  // Vector2 局部\n  if (/^[A-Za-z_]\\w*$/.test(s)) return localIR(s, 'vel', callLine, new Set());\n  return null;\n}\n\n// ============ 实例后处理（NewGore 之后对同一 gore 实例的赋值/缩放） ============\nconst goreIdx = new Map();     // 索引变量名 → 该 NewGore 发射的表项对象数组\nconst goreAlias = new Map();   // `Gore g = Main.gore[n]`/`Gore g = g2` 别名 → 索引变量名\nlet activeGoreEntries = [];    // 最近一次 NewGore 的表项（供 `Gore g = NewGore(...)` 直取）\n\n/** 主扫描里解析一行中的后处理语句（返回是否产生 post） */\nfunction handlePostLine(plain, lineNo) {\n  let m;\n  // 别名绑定：`Gore g = Main.gore[n];` / `Gore g = <别名>;` / `Gore g = Gore.NewGore...`（后者在 handleGoreCall 内）\n  if ((m = plain.match(/^Gore\\s+([A-Za-z_]\\w*)\\s*=\\s*Main\\.gore\\[([A-Za-z_]\\w*)\\]\\s*;$/))) {\n    goreAlias.set(m[1], m[2]);\n    return;\n  }\n  if ((m = plain.match(/^Gore\\s+([A-Za-z_]\\w*)\\s*=\\s*([A-Za-z_]\\w*)\\s*;$/)) && goreAlias.has(m[2])) {\n    goreAlias.set(m[1], goreAlias.get(m[2]));\n    return;\n  }\n  // 索引变量复用：`n = Main.gore[n]` 不存在；`n = Gore.NewGore(...)` 在 handleGoreCall 内重绑\n  // 目标解析：`Main.gore[n].field` / `alias.field`\n  const targetOf = (s) => {\n    let mm = s.match(/^Main\\.gore\\[([A-Za-z_]\\w*)\\]\\.(\\w+)$/);\n    if (mm) return { idx: mm[1], field: mm[2] };\n    mm = s.match(/^([A-Za-z_]\\w*)\\.(\\w+)$/);\n    if (mm && goreAlias.has(mm[1])) return { idx: goreAlias.get(mm[1]), field: mm[2] };\n    return null;\n  };\n  const stmts = plain.split(';').map((x) => x.trim()).filter(Boolean);\n  for (const st of stmts) {\n    // `<lhs> <op> <rhs>` 单语句\n    let mm = st.match(/^([A-Za-z_]\\w*(?:\\[[A-Za-z_]\\w*\\])?\\.[A-Za-z_]\\w*(?:\\.[A-Za-z_]\\w*)?)\\s*(=|\\*=|\\+=|-=)\\s*(.+)$/);\n    if (!mm) continue;\n    const tgt = targetOf(mm[1].replace(/\\[(\\w+)\\]/, '[$1]'));\n    if (!tgt) continue;\n    const list = goreIdx.get(tgt.idx);\n    if (!list) continue;\n    const op = compilePostOp(tgt.field, mm[2], mm[3], lineNo);\n    if (!op) { unresolvablePost.push({ line: lineNo, stmt: st }); continue; }\n    for (const e of list) (e.post ??= []).push(op);\n  }\n}\n\n/** 单条后处理语句 → op 数组（有序语义） */\nfunction compilePostOp(field, op, rhs, lineNo) {\n  const r = rhs.trim();\n  let m;\n  if (field === 'velocity' || field === 'scale' || field === 'alpha' || field === 'rotation' || field === 'position') {\n    if (op === '*=') {\n      if (field === 'velocity') {\n        const v = numLit(r);\n        if (v != null) return ['vm', v];\n        // `1f + (float)Next(-20,21) * 0.01f`（scale）在 scale 分支\n      }\n      if (field === 'scale') {\n        if ((m = r.match(/^([\\d.]+)[fF]?\\s*\\+\\s*\\(float\\)\\s*Main\\.rand\\.Next\\((-?\\d+),\\s*(-?\\d+)\\)\\s*\\*\\s*([\\d.]+)[fF]?$/))) {\n          const lo = +m[1] + (+m[2]) * +m[4], hi = +m[1] + (+m[3] - 1) * +m[4];\n          return ['sm', +lo.toFixed(4), +hi.toFixed(4)];\n        }\n        const v = numLit(r);\n        if (v != null) return ['sm', v, v];\n      }\n    }\n    if (op === '=') {\n      if (field === 'velocity') {\n        const ir = compileVel(r, lineNo);\n        if (ir) return ['vs', ir];\n        return null;\n      }\n      if (field === 'scale') { const v = numLit(r); if (v != null) return ['ss', v]; }\n      if (field === 'alpha') {\n        if (r === 'alpha') return ['al', 'npc'];\n        const v = numLit(r);\n        if (v != null) return ['al', v];\n      }\n      if (field === 'rotation') {\n        if (r === 'MathF.PI' || r === 'Math.PI') return ['ro', Math.PI];\n      }\n      if (field === 'position') {\n        const ir = compilePos(r, lineNo);\n        if (ir) return ['ps', ir];\n        return null;\n      }\n    }\n    return null;\n  }\n  // `velocity.X += / -=`、`velocity.Y -= Main.rand.NextFloat()`\n  if ((m = field.match(/^velocity\\.([XY])$/))) {\n    const axis = m[1] === 'X' ? 'x' : 'y';\n    if (op === '+=') {\n      const v = numLit(r);\n      if (v != null) return ['va', axis, v, v];\n      if ((m = r.match(/^\\(float\\)\\s*Main\\.rand\\.Next\\((-?\\d+),\\s*(-?\\d+)\\)\\s*\\*\\s*([\\d.]+)[fF]?$/))) {\n        const lo = +m[1] * +m[3], hi = (+m[2] - 1) * +m[3];\n        return ['va', axis, +lo.toFixed(4), +hi.toFixed(4)];\n      }\n    }\n    if (op === '-=') {\n      if (r === 'Main.rand.NextFloat()') return ['va', axis, -1, 0];\n      const v = numLit(r);\n      if (v != null) return ['va', axis, -v, -v];\n    }\n    return null;\n  }\n  return null;\n}\n\nfunction emitEntry(npcId, fields, line, gates, ai, loopN) {\n  if (!entries.has(npcId)) entries.set(npcId, []);\n  const f = { ...fields, line };\n  if (gates.length) f.gates = gates;\n  if (ai.length) f.ai = ai;\n  if (loopN && loopN > 1) f.loop = loopN;\n  entries.get(npcId).push(f);\n  return f;\n}\n\n// ---------- 逻辑行扫描（词法括号走查：对象初始化器 `{`/尾随 `}` 也计 depth） ----------\nconst LOOP_GUARD = {\n  kind: 'loop', cond: null, types: null, exclude: null, bounds: null, life: null,\n  gates: [], gateFree: null, staticTypes: null, loopGates: [], party: false, gateUnknown: [],\n  loopN: 1, loopVar: null,\n};\nfor (const { text, line: lineNo } of logical) {\n  let i = 0;\n  let guard = pendingGuard;   // 跨行：守卫头与 `{` 分行的挂起上下文\n  pendingGuard = null;\n\n  const plain = text.trim();\n  // 后处理语句（NewGore 之后对同一实例的赋值）——先于守卫扫描消费\n  if (/Main\\.gore\\[|^\\s*Gore\\s+\\w+\\s*=|\\w+\\.velocity|\\w+\\.scale|\\w+\\.alpha|\\w+\\.rotation|\\w+\\.position/.test(plain)) {\n    handlePostLine(plain, lineNo);\n  }\n\n  // 局部变量赋值流（声明与重赋值；供 `num + K` id 还原 + Vector2 局部 IR）\n  {\n    let m;\n    const isVecDecl = /^(?:int|float|double|byte|short|long|uint|var|bool|Vector2)\\s+([A-Za-z_]\\w*)\\s*=\\s*(.+?);$/.test(plain);\n    if ((m = plain.match(/^(?:int|float|double|byte|short|long|uint|var|bool|Vector2)\\s+([A-Za-z_]\\w*)\\s*=\\s*(.+?);$/))\n      || (m = plain.match(/^([A-Za-z_]\\w*)\\s*=(?![=>])\\s*(.+?);$/))) {\n      if (!/^(if|for|while|switch|return|else|do|try)$/.test(m[1])) {\n        const r = resolveChain(null);\n        recordLocalAssign(m[1], m[2], lineNo, r.typeCtxId);\n        // Vector2 局部 IR（pos 优先，vel 兜底；两者都失败则跳过）\n        if (/^Vector2\\s/.test(plain) || vecHist.has(m[1]) || /^(?:position|base\\.Center|base\\.Top|base\\.Bottom|new Vector2|Vector2\\.)/.test(m[2].trim())\n          || m[2].trim() === 'velocity') {\n          const pi = compilePos(m[2], lineNo);\n          if (pi) recordVec(m[1], 'pos', pi, lineNo);\n          else {\n            const vi = compileVel(m[2], lineNo);\n            if (vi) recordVec(m[1], 'vel', vi, lineNo);\n          }\n        }\n      }\n    }\n    // Vector2 局部突变：`v *= K`（含 `v.X *= -1f` 的 flipX 标记）\n    if ((m = plain.match(/^([A-Za-z_]\\w*)\\s*\\*=\\s*(-?[\\d.]+)[fF]?;$/))) {\n      const rec = vecIRFor(m[1], lineNo);\n      if (rec) recordVec(m[1], rec.kind, scaleIR(rec.ir.__ref ? {} : rec.ir, +m[2]), lineNo);\n    }\n    if ((m = plain.match(/^([A-Za-z_]\\w*)\\.X\\s*\\*=\\s*(-?[\\d.]+)[fF]?;$/))) {\n      const rec = vecIRFor(m[1], lineNo);\n      if (rec && rec.kind === 'vel' && rec.ir.rv) {\n        const [k, flip] = rec.ir.rv;\n        recordVec(m[1], 'vel', { rv: [k, flip + 1] }, lineNo);   // flip 次数累积（奇数次取反）\n      }\n    }\n    void isVecDecl;\n  }\n\n  while (i < text.length) {\n    const c = text[i];\n    if (c === '{') {\n      pushCtx(guard ?? null);\n      guard = null;\n      depth++;\n      i++;\n      continue;\n    }\n    if (c === '}') {\n      if (stack.length) stack.pop();\n      depth--;\n      for (const k of [...caseAt.keys()]) if (k > depth) caseAt.delete(k);\n      guard = null;\n      i++;\n      continue;\n    }\n    if (c === ' ' || c === '\\t' || c === ';') { i++; continue; }\n\n    // 语句起点：守卫头 / case 标签 / 普通语句段\n    const rest = text.slice(i);\n    let m;\n    let handled = true;\n    if ((m = rest.match(/^(else\\s+if|if)\\s*\\(/))) {\n      const cond = parenBody(rest, m[0].length - 1);\n      guard = makeGuard(m[1].replace(/\\s+/g, ''), cond);\n      i += m[0].length + cond.length + 1;\n    } else if ((m = rest.match(/^else\\b/))) {\n      guard = makeGuard('else', 'else');\n      i += m[0].length;\n    } else if ((m = rest.match(/^switch\\s*\\(/))) {\n      const cond = parenBody(rest, m[0].length - 1);\n      guard = makeGuard('switch', cond);\n      i += m[0].length + cond.length + 1;\n    } else if ((m = rest.match(/^case\\s+([^:]+):/))) {\n      const lbl = m[1].trim();\n      const e = evalIdExpr(lbl);\n      caseAt.set(depth, { label: lbl, types: e && e.kind === 'id' ? new Set([e.id]) : null });\n      i += m[0].length;\n    } else if ((m = rest.match(/^default\\s*:/))) {\n      caseAt.set(depth, null);\n      i += m[0].length;\n    } else if ((m = rest.match(/^(for|foreach|while|do|try)\\b/))) {\n      // for 头部：整数循环变量区间（id/pos 表达式引用时还原/展开）+ 常量次数（loop 字段）\n      const fh = rest.match(/^for\\s*\\(\\s*(?:int|float|double|var)?\\s*([A-Za-z_]\\w*)\\s*=\\s*(-?\\d+)\\s*;\\s*\\1\\s*(<=|<)\\s*(\\d+)\\s*;/);\n      let loopN = 1;\n      if (fh) {\n        const lo = +fh[2], hi = +fh[4];\n        const rg = [lo, fh[3] === '<=' ? hi : hi - 1];\n        recordLocalAssign(fh[1], `${rg[0]}`, lineNo, resolveChain(null).typeCtxId, rg);\n        const n = (fh[3] === '<=' ? hi - lo + 1 : hi - lo);\n        if (n > 0 && n <= 64) loopN = n;\n        guard = { ...LOOP_GUARD, loopN, loopVar: fh[1] };\n      }\n      // 头部条件括号整体跳过（否则会被当成语句段消费掉守卫）\n      const op = rest.indexOf('(');\n      if (op >= 0) {\n        const hd = parenBody(rest, op);\n        i += op + hd.length + 2;\n      } else {\n        i += m[0].length;\n      }\n    } else {\n      handled = false;\n    }\n    if (handled) continue;\n\n    // 普通语句段：吃到顶层 ; { } 为止；段内提取 Gore.NewGore* 调用（此时 depth 即调用点深度）\n    let j = i, pd = 0;\n    const segStart = i;\n    while (j < text.length) {\n      const ch = text[j];\n      if (ch === '(') pd++;\n      else if (ch === ')') pd--;\n      else if (pd === 0 && (ch === ';' || ch === '{' || ch === '}')) break;\n      j++;\n    }\n    const seg = text.slice(segStart, j);\n    const segLoopN = guard && guard.kind === 'loop' ? (guard.loopN ?? 1) : 1;\n    // NewGore 结果落地的局部变量（`int n = NewGore(...)` / `Gore g = NewGorePerfect(...)`）：\n    // 供后续 Main.gore[n]/别名后处理定位表项\n    let lhs = null;\n    {\n      const lm = seg.match(/^(?:int\\s+|Gore\\s+)?([A-Za-z_]\\w*)\\s*=\\s*Gore\\.NewGore(Perfect|Direct)?\\(/);\n      if (lm && seg.indexOf('Gore.NewGore') === lm[0].length - 'Gore.NewGore'.length - (lm[2] ? lm[2].length : 0) - 1) lhs = lm[1];\n      else if (lm) lhs = lm[1];\n    }\n    let from = 0;\n    for (;;) {\n      const at = seg.indexOf('Gore.NewGore', from);\n      if (at < 0) break;\n      from = at + 1;\n      const p = seg.indexOf('(', at);\n      if (p < 0) continue;\n      const callee = seg.slice(at, p);\n      if (!/^Gore\\.NewGore(Perfect|Direct)?$/.test(callee)) continue;\n      handleGoreCall(callee, splitArgs(parenBody(seg, p)), lineNo, segLoopN, lhs);\n      lhs = null;   // 同段多调用：只绑首个（源码无此形态）\n    }\n    if (guard && guard.kind === 'loop') guard = null; // 无括号体 = 单语句，守卫就此消费\n    i = j;\n  }\n  // 行尾仍未消费的守卫头（与 `{` 分行）挂到下一逻辑行\n  if (guard) pendingGuard = guard;\n}\n\n/** 单个 Gore.NewGore* 调用 → 表项 */\nfunction handleGoreCall(callee, args, lineNo, segLoopN = 1, lhs = null) {\n  callsTotal++;\n  const a = namedArgs(args);\n  if (a.Position === undefined || a.Type === undefined) return;\n  const cc = caseAt.get(depth) ?? caseAt.get(depth - 1) ?? null;\n  if (process.env.GORE_DBG_LINE && lineNo === +process.env.GORE_DBG_LINE) {\n    console.error('[dbg] line', lineNo, 'depth', depth, 'stack', JSON.stringify(stack.map((c2) => c2?.cond)), 'case', cc?.label ?? null);\n  }\n  const r = resolveChain(cc ? {\n    id: 0, kind: 'case', cond: `case ${cc.label}`, types: cc.types, exclude: null, bounds: null,\n    life: null, gates: [], gateFree: null, staticTypes: null, loopGates: [], party: false,\n    gateUnknown: [], loopN: null, loopVar: null,\n  } : null);\n  r.loopN *= segLoopN;\n\n  if (r.party) { callsPartySkipped++; return; }                 // 派对帽专属段\n  if (r.life === 'alive') { callsHitSkipped++; return; }        // hit 段\n  for (const u of r.gateUnknown) unresolvedGates.push({ line: lineNo, cond: u });\n\n  // 循环相位门（`num709 < 3`）：常量循环域内前 K 次迭代 → 折算 loop 次数，不进 gates\n  for (const lg of r.loopGates) {\n    const n = r.loopMap.get(lg.var);\n    if (n && (lg.op === '<' ? lg.v : lg.v + 1) < n) {\n      r.loopN = Math.max(1, Math.round((r.loopN / n) * (lg.op === '<' ? lg.v : lg.v + 1)));\n      continue;\n    }\n    unresolvedGates.push({ line: lineNo, cond: `${lg.var} ${lg.op} ${lg.v}` });\n  }\n\n  // 循环变量展开：pos/vel/scale 表达式引用循环域 ≤8 的变量 → 逐值生成条目（loop 归 1）\n  const posRaw = String(a.Position ?? 'position');\n  const velRaw = String(a.Velocity ?? 'velocity');\n  const sclRaw = String(a.Scale ?? '');\n  const unrollVars = [...r.loopMap.entries()].filter(([v, n]) => n >= 1 && n <= 8\n    && (new RegExp(`\\\\b${v}\\\\b`).test(posRaw) || new RegExp(`\\\\b${v}\\\\b`).test(velRaw) || new RegExp(`\\\\b${v}\\\\b`).test(sclRaw)));\n  let variants = [{}];\n  let unrollCount = 1;\n  if (unrollVars.length) {\n    variants = [{}];\n    for (const [v, n] of unrollVars) {\n      const lo = Number((locals.get(v) ?? []).slice(-1)[0]?.valExpr ?? 0);\n      const next = [];\n      for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) for (const cur of variants) next.push({ ...cur, [v]: String(lo + k) });\n      variants = next;\n      unrollCount *= n;\n    }\n    r.loopN = Math.max(1, Math.round(r.loopN / unrollCount));\n  }\n\n  const emit = (sub) => {\n    const posExprStr = Object.keys(sub).length\n      ? posRaw.replace(/\\b([A-Za-z_]\\w*)\\b/g, (w) => sub[w] ?? w)\n      : posRaw;\n    const velExprStr = Object.keys(sub).length\n      ? velRaw.replace(/\\b([A-Za-z_]\\w*)\\b/g, (w) => sub[w] ?? w)\n      : velRaw;\n    const sclExprStr = Object.keys(sub).length\n      ? sclRaw.replace(/\\b([A-Za-z_]\\w*)\\b/g, (w) => sub[w] ?? w)\n      : sclRaw;\n    emitCallEntry(callee, { Position: posExprStr, Velocity: velExprStr, Scale: sclExprStr, Type: a.Type }, lineNo, r, lhs);\n  };\n  for (const sub of variants) emit(sub);\n}\n\n/** 单变体表项发射（gates 按 freeTypes 逐型豁免；不可求值 id 登记 unresolved） */\nfunction emitCallEntry(callee, a, lineNo, r, lhs) {\n  const velFields = (() => {\n    const v = String(a.Velocity ?? 'velocity');\n    const c = compileVel(v, lineNo);\n    if (c) return c;\n    return { velExpr: v }; // 不可编译 → 运行时按继承 NPC 速度\n  })();\n  const scaleFields = (() => {\n    const v = a.Scale;\n    if (v === undefined || v === '1f' || v === '1') return {};\n    let m;\n    if ((m = String(v).match(/^([\\d.]+)[fF]?$/))) return { scale: +m[1] };\n    return { scaleExpr: String(v) }; // 'scale' = NPC.scale；其余登记原文\n  })();\n  const posFields = (() => {\n    if (a.Position === 'position') return {};\n    const c = compilePos(a.Position, lineNo);\n    if (c) return c;\n    return { posExpr: String(a.Position) }; // 不可编译 → 运行时按左上角\n  })();\n  const perfect = callee === 'Gore.NewGorePerfect' ? { perfect: true } : {};\n  const base = { ...velFields, ...scaleFields, ...posFields, ...perfect };\n\n  // id 表达式直接引用外层循环变量（如 `num343`）= 每次迭代取一个具体 id，\n  // 区间/候选已编码迭代序列 → loop 归 1，防运行时重复随机取样\n  if (r.loopN > 1 && [...r.loopMap.keys()].some((v) => new RegExp(`\\\\b${v}\\\\b`).test(String(a.Type)))) r.loopN = 1;\n\n  let e = null;\n  let perType = null;\n  let fields = null;\n\n  if (r.types) {\n    const target = [...r.types].filter((t) => !r.exclude.has(t));\n    const res = resolveIdPerType(String(a.Type), target, lineNo, r.typeCtxId);\n    if (res?.fields) fields = res.fields;\n    else if (res?.perType) perType = res.perType;\n    // 区间/候选尺寸 == 循环次数 = 序列已被区间编码（469-475×7 等）→ loop 归 1\n    const span = fields ? (fields.ids ? fields.ids.length : fields.idMin != null ? (fields.idMax - fields.idMin + 1) : 0) : 0;\n    if (span > 1 && span === r.loopN) r.loopN = 1;\n  }\n\n  if (!r.types) {\n    defaultEntries.push({ line: lineNo, idExpr: String(a.Type), gates: r.gates });\n    return;\n  }\n  const target = [...r.types].filter((t) => !r.exclude.has(t));\n  const emitted = [];\n\n  if (perType) {\n    for (const t of target) {\n      emitted.push(emitEntry(t, { ...base, ...perType.get(t) }, lineNo, r.gateFree.has(t) ? [] : r.gates, r.ai, r.loopN));\n    }\n  } else if (fields) {\n    for (const t of target) {\n      emitted.push(emitEntry(t, { ...base, ...fields }, lineNo, r.gateFree.has(t) ? [] : r.gates, r.ai, r.loopN));\n    }\n  } else {\n    for (const t of target) {\n      emitted.push(emitEntry(t, { ...base, expr: String(a.Type) }, lineNo, r.gateFree.has(t) ? [] : r.gates, r.ai, r.loopN));\n    }\n    unresolved.push({\n      line: lineNo, expr: String(a.Type),\n      types: target.length > 6 ? `${target[0]}..${target[target.length - 1]}(${target.length})` : target.join('|'),\n    });\n  }\n  // 后处理定位：`int n = NewGore(...)` / `Gore g = NewGorePerfect(...)`（别名即时绑定）\n  if (lhs) {\n    goreIdx.set(lhs, emitted);\n    goreAlias.set(lhs, lhs);\n  }\n  activeGoreEntries = emitted;\n  void e;\n}\n\n// ============ 手工补丁：守卫链为排除式(`type != N` + return)的收尾支 ============\n// catch-all else 尾支（NPC.cs:90859-90867）：`if (type != 156) return;` → 仅 Red Devil\n// 走到 184/185/185。排除式守卫无法从链上还原出正集（需要整链 else 语义），按源行手工钉死。\nconst MANUAL_PATCHES = {\n  156: [\n    { id: 184, line: 90865 },\n    { id: 185, line: 90866 },\n    { id: 185, line: 90867 },\n  ],\n};\nfor (const [npcId, list] of Object.entries(MANUAL_PATCHES)) {\n  if (!entries.has(+npcId)) entries.set(+npcId, []);\n  entries.get(+npcId).push(...list);\n}\n\n// ============ 贴图尺寸表（出现过的 gore id） ============\nconst idsSeen = new Set();\nfor (const list of entries.values()) {\n  for (const e of list) {\n    if (e.id != null) idsSeen.add(e.id);\n    if (e.idAlt != null) idsSeen.add(e.idAlt);\n    if (e.idMin != null) for (let i = e.idMin; i <= (e.idMax ?? 'w' : 'h';\n  const v = evalArith(s.replace(/[WH]/g, '1'));\n  return v == null ? null : { k: v, dim };\n}\n\n/** 单个轴项 → { off, f, r:[a,b] } | null（f=尺寸分数；r=随机尺寸分数域） */\nfunction axisTerm(t, dim) {\n  const term = t.trim();\n  let m;\n  if ((m = term.match(/^-?\\d+\\.?\\d*[fFdD]?$/))) return { off: parseFloat(term.replace(/[fFdD]$/, '')) };\n  // `(<和式>) * 常数` / `<和式> * 尺寸分数` → 递归缩放（vector30 的 (float)(-w + (int)(w*u))*0.5 等）\n  if ((m = term.match(/^\\((.+)\\)\\s*\\*\\s*(.+)$/)) && splitTop(term, '|').length === 1 && !term.includes('NextFloat()')) {\n    const inner = axisExpr(m[1], dim);\n    const c = coeffOf(m[2]);\n    if (!inner || !c || c.dim === (dim === 'width' ? 'h' : 'w')) return null;\n    if (!inner.f && !inner.off && !inner.r) return null;\n    if (inner.r && c.k !== 1) return null;\n    return {\n      ...(inner.off != null ? { off: inner.off * (c.dim == null ? c.k : NaN) } : {}),\n      ...(inner.f != null ? { f: inner.f * c.k } : {}),\n      ...(inner.r ? { r: [inner.r[0] * c.k, inner.r[1] * c.k] } : {}),\n    };\n  }\n  // 尺寸线性项\n  const c = coeffOf(term);\n  if (c) {\n    if (c.dim === dim) return { f: c.k };\n    if (c.dim == null) return { off: c.k };\n    return null;\n  }\n  // `<尺寸式> * Main.rand.NextFloat()` → 随机分数域\n  if ((m = term.match(/^(.+?)\\s*\\*\\s*Main\\.rand\\.NextFloat\\(\\)$/))) {\n    const cc = coeffOf(m[1]);\n    if (!cc || cc.dim !== dim) return null;\n    return { r: [0, cc.k] };\n  }\n  // `(int)((float)width * Main.rand.NextFloat())` → 随机分数域 [0,1]（截断粒度 ≤1px，近似）\n  if ((m = stripCasts(term).match(/^\\(\\s*(width|height)\\s*\\*\\s*Main\\.rand\\.NextFloat\\(\\)\\s*\\)$/))) {\n    if (m[1] !== dim) return null;\n    return { r: [0, 1] };\n  }\n  if ((m = term.match(/^Main\\.rand\\.Next\\(\\s*(width|height)\\s*\\)$/))) {\n    if (m[1] !== dim) return null;\n    return { r: [0, 1] };\n  }\n  return null;\n}\n\n/** 轴分量编译 → { off, f, r:[a,b] } | null（空表达式 = 无偏移） */\nfunction axisExpr(expr, dim) {\n  const body = stripCasts(String(expr)).trim();\n  if (!body) return {};\n  const terms = splitTerms(body);\n  const out = { off: 0, f: 0, r: null };\n  for (const { t, sign } of terms) {\n    const r = axisTerm(t, dim);\n    if (!r) return null;\n    if (r.off != null) out.off += sign * r.off;\n    if (r.f != null) out.f += sign * r.f;\n    if (r.r) out.r = addRange(out.r, sign > 0 ? r.r : [sign * r.r[1], sign * r.r[0]]);\n  }\n  const res = {};\n  if (out.off) res.off = +out.off.toFixed(4);\n  if (out.f) res.f = +out.f.toFixed(4);\n  if (out.r) res.r = [+out.r[0].toFixed(4), +out.r[1].toFixed(4)];\n  return res;\n}"

 "old_string": "/** IR 数值缩放（局部向量 × 系数） */\nfunction scaleIR(ir, k) {\n  const out = {};\n  for (const key of ['ox', 'oy', 'fx', 'fy', 'dx', 'dy']) {\n    if (ir[key] != null) out[key] = +(ir[key] * k).toFixed(4);\n  }\n  for (const key of ['rwx', 'rwy', 'jx', 'jy']) {\n    if (ir[key]) out[key] = [+(ir[key][0] * k).toFixed(4), +(ir[key][1] * k).toFixed(4)];\n  }\n  if (ir.dc) out.dc = [+(ir.dc[0] * Math.abs(k)).toFixed(4), +(ir.dc[1] * Math.abs(k)).toFixed(4)];\n  if (ir.rot) out.rot = ir.rot.map((r) => ({ ...r, k: +(r.k * k).toFixed(4) }));\n  return out;\n}",
 "new_string": "/** IR 缩放（局部向量 × 系数；系数可为尺寸分数 {k,dim}——rot 项的 k 继承 dim） */\nfunction scaleIR(ir, coeff) {\n  const { k, dim } = typeof coeff === 'number' ? { k: coeff, dim: null } : coeff;\n  const out = {};\n  for (const key of ['ox', 'oy', 'dx', 'dy']) {\n    if (ir[key] != null) out[key] = +(ir[key] * k).toFixed(4);\n  }\n  if (ir.fx != null && !(dim === 'h')) out.fx = +(ir.fx * (dim === 'w' ? k : k)).toFixed(4);\n  if (ir.fy != null && !(dim === 'w')) out.fy = +(ir.fy * k).toFixed(4);\n  for (const key of ['rwx', 'rwy', 'jx', 'jy']) {\n    if (ir[key]) out[key] = [+(ir[key][0] * k).toFixed(4), +(ir[key][1] * k).toFixed(4)];\n  }\n  if (ir.dc) out.dc = [+(ir.dc[0] * Math.abs(k)).toFixed(4), +(ir.dc[1] * Math.abs(k)).toFixed(4)];\n  if (ir.rot) {\n    out.rot = ir.rot.map((r) => {\n      if (dim && r.dim) return null;   // 双重尺寸分数不支撑\n      return { ...r, k: +(r.k * k).toFixed(4), dim: r.dim ?? dim ?? null };\n    });\n    if (out.rot.some((r) => r == null)) return null;\n  }\n  if (ir.rv) out.rv = [+(ir.rv[0] * k).toFixed(4), ir.rv[1]];\n  return out;\n}"
}

```
function coeffOf(kexpr) {
  const s = stripCasts(String(kexpr))
    .replace(/\bwidth\b/g, 'W').replace(/\bheight\b/g, 'H');
  const hasW = s.includes('W'), hasH = s.includes('H');
  if (!hasW && !hasH) {
    const v = evalArith(s);
    return v == null ? null : { k: v, dim: null };
  }
  if (hasW && hasH) return null;
  const dim = hasW ? 'w' : 'h';
  const v = evalArith(s.replace(/[WH]/g, '1'));
  return v == null ? null : { k: v, dim };
}

/** 单个轴项 → { off, f, r:[a,b] } | null（dim ∈ 'w'|'h'；f

```

 "new_string": "    const comp = (c, axis) => {\n      const t = c.trim();\n      let mm;\n      if ((mm = t.match(/^(-?[\\d.]+)[fF]?$/))) { out[`v${axis}`] = +mm[1]; return true; }\n      if (t === 'hitDirection') { out.hd = 1; return true; }\n      // `Next(a,b) * C… * (float)hitDirection` → 随机幅值 × 击退方向\n      if ((mm = t.match(/^\\(?float\\)?\\s*Main\\.rand\\.Next\\((-?\\d+),\\s*(-?\\d+)\\)((?:\\s*\\*\\s*-?[\\d.]+[fFdD]?)+)\\s*\\*\\s*\\(float\\)\\s*hitDirection$/))) {\n        let mag = 1;\n        for (const cc of mm[3].matchAll(/([\\d.]+)[fFdD]?/g)) mag *= +cc[1];\n        out.hdm = [+mm[1] * mag, (+mm[2] - 1) * mag];\n        return true;\n      }\n      if ((mm = t.match(/^\\(?float\\)?\\s*Main\\.rand\\.Next\\((-?\\d+),\\s*(-?\\d+)\\)((?:\\s*\\*\\s*-?[\\d.]+[fFdD]?)*)$/)) && mm[3]) {\n        let mag = 1;\n        for (const cc of mm[3].matchAll(/([\\d.]+)[fFdD]?/g)) mag *= +cc[1];\n        const lo = +mm[1] * mag, hi = (+mm[2] - 1) * mag;\n        out[`m${axis}`] = +((lo + hi) / 2).toFixed(4);\n        out[`a${axis}`] = +((hi - lo) / 2).toFixed(4);\n        return true;\n      }\n      if ((mm = t.match(/^Main\\.rand\\.Next\\((-?\\d+),\\s*(-?\\d+)\\)$/))) {\n        const lo = +mm[1], hi = +mm[2] - 1;\n        out[`m${axis}`] = +((lo + hi) / 2).toFixed(4);\n        out[`a${axis}`] = +((hi - lo) / 2).toFixed(4);\n        return true;\n      }\n      if ((mm = t.match(/^Main\\.rand\\.Next\\((-?\\d+)\\)$/))) {\n        out[`m${axis}`] = +((+mm[1] - 1) / 2).toFixed(4);\n        out[`a${axis}`] = +((+mm[1] - 1) / 2).toFixed(4);\n        return true;\n      }\n      if ((mm = t.match(/^Main\\.rand\\.NextFloat\\(\\)\\s*\\*\\s*([\\d.]+)[fF]?$/))) {\n        out[`m${axis}`] = +((+mm[1]) / 2).toFixed(4);\n        out[`a${axis}`] = +((+mm[1]) / 2).toFixed(4);\n        return true;\n      }\n      // `0f - (3f + Next(4)*0.3f)` 之类复合 → 项式代数（off ± j 抖动域折叠成 m/a）\n      const ax = axisExpr(t, axis === 'x' ? 'w' : 'h');\n      if (!ax || ax.f != null || ax.r != null) return false;\n      if (ax.off != null && ax.j == null) { out[`v${axis}`] = ax.off; return true; }\n      if (ax.j != null) {\n        const lo = (ax.off ?? 0) + Math.min(ax.j[0], ax.j[1]);\n        const hi = (ax.off ?? 0) + Math.max(ax.j[0], ax.j[1]);\n        out[`m${axis}`] = +((lo + hi) / 2).toFixed(4);\n        out[`a${axis}`] = +((hi - lo) / 2).toFixed(4);\n        return true;\n      }\n      return false;\n    };"

_meta.notes）\n//   物理：Gore.Update（Gore.cs:313-1102）——本表能产出的家族全实现：\n//     · 默认粘滞碎块：重力 0.2 + rotation += vx*0.05（:762-763）→ 碰撞盒\n//       min(w,h)*0.9*scale（:1000-1014）→ 落地 vx*=0.97（:1015-1029）→\n//       timeLeft -= DisappearSpeed / alpha += DisappearSpeedAlpha（:1030-1037）\n//     · 血块收缩族 {11,12,13,16,17,61,62,63,99,220-222,375-377,435-437,861-862}\n//       非粘滞：v*=0.98 + scale-=0.007/0.01（:535-556）+ alpha += 2*AlphaSpeed（:1041）\n//     · 276-282（庆典史莱姆彩带）：v*=0.98 + vy<scale 时 +0.05 + ±0.01 翻滚（:352-371）\n//       叠加默认重力支与粘滞碰撞\n//     · SpecialAI 1 帆降族（860/892/893 海盗船残骸、1014-1016 黑魔法师长袍）：\n//       Gore_UpdateSail（:1231-1291）相位下落 + 碰撞转落地滑移\n//     · SpecialAI 7（1218 暗影宝箱怪钱币）：UpdateLightningBunnySparks（:244-262）\n//       timeLeft 倒数 + alpha = lerp(255,0,timeLeft/15)\n//     · 16/17 火苗族（NewGore :1487-1490）：alpha=100 + scale=0.7 + light=1 →\n//       Update :546-556 走 0.01 收缩档 → 尾段 :1078-1101 light*scale 衰减通道\n//       AddLight（16 = 橙火 (1,0.8,0.3)，17 = 蓝火 (0.3,0.6,1)）；\n//       GetAlpha :1487-1490 返回 Color(255,255,255,0)——XNA 预乘混合下 A=0 即\n//       加性全亮（1405 同处为取场景光照 RGB，见 Gore.cs:1164-1174，此处从 1456）\n//   出生：Gore.NewGore（:1306-1482）——vy -= rand(10,31)*0.1 / vx += rand(-20,21)*0.1\n//   随机扰动（:1347-1348）、ChildSafety 替换（:1339-1345）、粘滞/时档（:1361-1374）、\n//   600 槽池（:1328-1340）。\n//   落叶(SpecialAI 3)/滴水族不出自 HitEffect，由 render/NatureParticles 独占。\nimport { Entity } from './Entity';\nimport type { GameHooks } from './types';\nimport { moveAndCollide } from '../physics/TileCollision';\nimport goreTable from '../data/vanilla-gore.json';\n\n/** Gore.goreTime（Gore.cs:16） */\nexport const GORE_TIME = 600;\n/** Main.gore 池容量（Gore.cs:1328-1340：无空槽则丢弃） */\nconst MAX_GORE = 600;\n/** 纹理缺失时的碰撞盒回退（Gore.cs:999 num36=32） */\nconst FALLBACK_TEX = 32;\n/** 1 世界像素 = 1/16 格 */\nconst PX = 1 / 16;\n\n/** NewGore :1361-1364 sticky=false 族（goreTime!=0 时） */\nconst NON_STICKY_TYPES = new Set([11, 12, 13, 16, 17, 61, 62, 63, 99, 220, 221, 222, 435, 436, 437, 861, 862]);\n/** 血块收缩族（Gore.cs:535-556；16/17 独立支为 0.01 档） */\nconst SHRINK_TYPES = new Set([11, 12, 13, 61, 62, 63, 99, 220, 221, 222, 375, 376, 377, 435, 436, 437, 861, 862]);\n/** 彩带阻尼族（Gore.cs:352-371） */\nconst isDampRibbon = (t: number) => t >= 276 && t <= 282;\n/** GoreID.Sets.SpecialAI==1（GoreID.cs:9）：帆降 */\nconst SAIL_TYPES = new Set([860, 892, 893, 1014, 1015, 1016]);\n/** 多帧行数（NewGore :1391-1398：1201/1208→4、1217/1218→3；表内仅 1218 实际出现） */\nconst FRAME_ROWS: Record<number, number> = { 1201: 4, 1208: 4, 1217: 3, 1218: 3, 1225: 3 };\n/** GoreID.Sets.DisappearSpeed（GoreID.cs:11，默认 1） */\nconst DISAPPEAR_SPEED: Record<number, number> = { 1007: 3, 1008: 10, 1024: 7, 1025: 7, 1026: 7 };\n/** GoreID.Sets.DisappearSpeedAlpha（GoreID.cs:13，默认 1） */\nconst DISAPPEAR_ALPHA: Record<number, number> = { 1007: 3, 1008: 10, 1024: 7, 1025: 7, 1026: 7, 1218: 3 };\nconst vanishSpeed = (t: number) => DISAPPEAR_SPEED[t] ?? 1;\nconst vanishAlpha = (t: number) => DISAPPEAR_ALPHA[t] ?? 1;\n\n/** ChildSafety.SafeGore（ChildSafety.cs：开启童模时危险 gore → 11-13 落叶替代） */\nconst SAFE_GORE = new Set([\n  11, 12, 13, 16, 17, 42, 44, 51, 52, 53, 54, 55, 56, 57, 61, 62, 63, 67, 68, 69, 99, 106,\n  120, 130, 131, 147, 148, 149, 150, 156, 166, 167, 168, 169, 170, 171, 172, 173, 174, 197,\n  198, 199, 200, 201, 202, 203, 204, 213, 217, 218, 219, 220, 221, 222, 257, 265, 266, 267,\n  268, 269, 276, 277, 278, 279, 280, 281, 282, 314, 315, 316, 321, 322, 326, 331, 360, 361,\n  362, 363, 364, 365, 366, 367, 368, 369, 370, 375, 376, 377, 406, 407, 408, 409, 410, 411,\n  412, 413, 414, 415, 416, 417, 418, 419, 420, 421, 422, 423, 424, 425, 426, 427, 428, 429,\n  430, 435, 436, 437, 521, 522, 523, 525, 526, 527, 542, 570, 571, 572, 580, 581, 582, 603,\n  604, 605, 606, 610, 611, 612, 613, 614, 615, 616, 617, 618, 639, 660, 661, 695, 696, 697,\n  698, 699, 700, 701, 702, 703, 704, 705, 706, 707, 708, 709, 710, 711, 712, 713, 714, 715,\n  716, 717, 718, 719, 720, 721, 728, 729, 730, 731, 732, 733, 734, 770, 825, 826, 827, 848,\n  849, 850, 851, 853, 854, 855, 856, 857, 858, 859, 860, 861, 862, 892, 893, 898, 899, 907,\n  908, 909, 910, 911, 912, 913, 914, 915, 916, 917, 918, 919, 920, 921, 922, 923, 924, 925,\n  926, 939, 940, 941, 942, 943, 964, 965, 966, 970, 971, 972, 973, 992, 1007, 1008, 1024,\n  1025, 1026, 1027, 1028, 1029, 1030, 1060, 1066, 1087, 1088, 1089, 1090, 1091, 1092, 1093,\n  1113, 1114, 1115, 1116, 1117, 1118, 1119, 1120, 1121, 1122, 1123, 1124, 1125, 1126, 1127,\n  1128, 1129, 1130, 1131, 1132, 1133, 1134, 1135, 1136, 1137, 1138, 1139, 1140, 1141, 1142,\n  1143, 1144, 1145, 1146, 1147, 1160, 1161, 1162, 1201, 1202, 1203, 1204, 1205, 1206, 1207,\n  1208, 1209, 1210, 1211, 1212, 1213, 1218, 1225, 1226, 1248, 1249, 1250, 1251, 1252, 1253,\n  1254, 1255, 1257, 1258, 1261, 1269, 1278, 1333, 1334, 1346, 1361, 1372, 1373, 1374, 1375,\n  1376, 1383, 1394, 1395, 1396, 1397, 1398, 1399, 1400, 1401, 1402,\n]);\n\n/**\n * ChildSafety.Disabled（ChildSafety.cs：原版默认 true = 关闭）。开启童模\n * （false）后 DangerousGore 一律替换成 11-13 落叶 + 半随机 scale + 速度减半\n * （Gore.NewGore :1339-1345），替换发生在粘滞/时档判定之前（后续分支按新 type 走）。\n */\nexport const ChildSafety = {\n  disabled: true,\n  /** DangerousGore(id)（ChildSafety.cs） */\n  dangerous(id: number): boolean {\n    return !SAFE_GORE.has(id);\n  },\n};\n\n/**\n * Main.netMode（Main.cs：0 单人 / 1 多人客户端 / 2 服务端）。单人游戏恒 0；\n * 联机会话由 Game 侧在进房/建房时切换（表内 `net` 门 = 原版 `Main.netMode != 1`\n * 一类守卫，客户端不本地产 gore——原版由服务端同步）。\n */\nlet goreNetMode = 0;\nexport function setGoreNetMode(mode: 0 | 1 | 2): void {\n  goreNetMode = mode;\n}\nexport function goreNetModeValue(): number {\n  return goreNetMode;\n}\n\n/** vanilla-gore.json 表项（字段语义见提取器 _meta.notes） */\nexport interface GoreGate {\n  k: 'alt' | 'sdir' | 'dmg' | 'shim' | 'net' | 'rnd' | 'redhat' | 'skip';\n  op?: '==' | '!=' | '>=' | '<=' | '>' | '<';\n  v?: number;\n  n?: number;\n  not?: boolean;\n  reason?: string;\n}\nexport interface GoreRotTerm {\n  /** Unit 轴：'x' | 'y' */\n  a: 'x' | 'y';\n  /** 符号：常数或 'sd' = (spriteDirection != 1 ? 1 : -1)（荷兰人 vector20） */\n  s: number | 'sd';\n  /** 系数（dim 非空时为尺寸分数） */\n  k: number;\n  dim?: 'w' | 'h' | null;\n}\nexport type GorePostOp =\n  | ['vm', number]\n  | ['va', 'x' | 'y', number, number]\n  | ['vs', Record<string, number | number[] | GoreRotTerm[]>]\n  | ['ss', number]\n  | ['sm', number, number]\n  | ['al', number | 'npc']\n  | ['ro', number]\n  | ['ps', Record<string, unknown>];\n\nexport interface GoreSpawnEntry {\n  id?: number | null;\n  /** `IsShimmerVariant ? idAlt : id`（微光变体 id） */\n  idAlt?: number;\n  idMin?: number; idMax?: number; ids?: number[]; n?: number;\n  /** 常量速度 */\n  vx?: number; vy?: number;\n  /** NPC 速度系数（velocity * k） */\n  kx?: number; ky?: number;\n  /** 均匀随机速度 [m-a, m+a]（Main.rand.Next 区间编译产物） */\n  mx?: number; my?: number; ax?: number; ay?: number;\n  /** 随机幅值 × 击退方向（`Next(a,b)*C*hitDirection`）→ [lo, hi] × hitDir */\n  hdm?: [number, number];\n  /** 均匀随机方向定速（UnitY.RotatedByRandom(2π)*K / Transform((K,0), rot(rand*2π))） */\n  de?: number;\n  /** 椭圆盘随机速度（NextVector2Circular(rx, ry)：单位向量 × 半径 × u） */\n  dc?: [number, number];\n  /** rotation.ToRotationVector2()*k（flip 奇数次 = spriteDirection==1 时 X 取反） */\n  rv?: [number, number];\n  /** vx 取击退方向（new Vector2(hitDirection, 0f)） */\n  hd?: number;\n  /** 出生点 = npc 左上角 + off + frac*[w,h] + rwd*[w,h] + jitter + direction*d + rot 项 + dc */\n  ox?: number; oy?: number; fx?: number; fy?: number;\n  /** 随机尺寸分数域 [a,b]（`Next(width)`/`(w±c)*NextFloat()` 编译产物） */\n  rwx?: [number, number]; rwy?: [number, number];\n  /** 像素抖动域 [a,b]（Utils.RandomVector2 / Next 常数链乘） */\n  jx?: [number, number]; jy?: [number, number];\n  /** ±direction 偏移（UnitX/UnitY × k × direction） */\n  dx?: number; dy?: number;\n  /** Unit 轴 × 系数再按 NPC rotation 旋转（(-UnitY*50).RotatedBy(rotation) 等） */\n  rot?: GoreRotTerm[];\n  scale?: number; scaleExpr?: string;\n  /** 未编译的原文（登记用） */\n  posExpr?: string; velExpr?: string; expr?: string;\n  /** 原版 ai[N]==V 门（不满足跳过该条） */\n  ai?: Array<{ slot: number; value: number; not?: boolean }>;\n  /** 结构化守卫门（运行时求值，不满足跳过该条） */\n  gates?: GoreGate[];\n  /** NewGore 之后的实例后处理（有序） */\n  post?: GorePostOp[];\n  /** Gore.NewGorePerfect：精确 pos/vel（随机扰动后回写，:1293-1299） */\n  perfect?: boolean;\n  /** 原版常量次数 for 循环体（如 EoC `for(<2)` 4 调用 = 8 块）；id 区间已编码迭代序列时为 1 */\n  loop?: number;\n  line?: number;\n}\n\n/** 死亡来源 NPC 的最小形状（Enemy/Critter 均满足；测试可传 mock） */\nexport interface GoreNpcSource {\n  vanillaId: number | null;\n  x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number;\n  vx: number; vy: number;\n  vanillaScale: number;\n  ai0: number; ai1: number; ai2: number; ai3: number;\n  hitDir?: number;\n  /** NPC.altTexture（变体贴图槽，缺省 0） */\n  altTexture?: number;\n  /** NPC.IsShimmerVariant（NPC.cs:6643：城镇 NPC 微光变体态，缺省 false） */\n  isShimmerVariant?: boolean;\n  /** NPC.spriteDirection（缺省 1；本仓 Enemy.facing 同符号约定） */\n  spriteDirection?: number;\n  /** NPC.direction（移动方向，缺省取 spriteDirection） */\n  direction?: number;\n  /** NPC.rotation（缺省 0；rot 项按此旋转） */\n  rotation?: number;\n  /** NPC.localAI[3]（RedHatSkeletronAdjustmentsEnabled 用，缺省 0） */\n  lai3?: number;\n  /** HitEffect dmg 实参（死亡钩缺省 9999 = 原版脚本化死亡调用约定） */\n  dmg?: number;\n  /** NPC.alpha（post `al:'npc'` 用，缺省 0） */\n  alpha?: number;\n}\n\ntype GoreTable = Record<string, GoreSpawnEntry[]> & {\n  _meta: { tex: Record<string, { w: number; h: number }>; [k: string]: unknown };\n};\nconst TABLE = goreTable as unknown as GoreTable;\nconst TEX = TABLE._meta?.tex ?? {};\n\n/** NPC id → 死亡 gore 表项（无表项 = 原版只出尘不出块，如史莱姆族） */\nexport function deathGoreFor(npcId: number): GoreSpawnEntry[] {\n  return TABLE[String(npcId)] ?? [];\n}\n\nconst irand = (n: number) => Math.floor(Math.random() * n);\n/** Main.rand.Next(min, max) 语义：[min, max-1] */\nconst irand2 = (min: number, max: number) => min + Math.floor(Math.random() * (max - min));\nconst uniform = (m: number, a: number) => m + (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * a;\n/** 区间均匀取值 [lo, hi]（端点闭合近似） */\nconst range01 = (r: readonly [number, number]) => r[0] + Math.random() * (r[1] - r[0]);\n/** Utils.NextVector2Circular（Utils.cs:1269）：单位向量 × (rx,ry) × u */\nfunction disk(rx: number, ry: number): { x: number; y: number } {\n  const a = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;\n  const u = Math.random();\n  return { x: Math.cos(a) * rx * u, y: Math.sin(a) * ry * u };\n}\n\n/** 存活 gore 池（= Main.gore 600 槽；渲染层 drawGorePieces 也从这里取） */\nconst livePool = new Set<GorePiece>();\n/** 切世界/重载时清池（EntityManager.clear 会丢弃引用但不置 dead，计数须同步归零） */\nexport function resetGorePool(): void {\n  livePool.clear();\n}\n/** 当前存活 gore（渲染/诊断用） */\nexport function liveGorePieces(): GorePiece[] {\n  return [...livePool];\n}\n\nexport class GorePiece extends Entity {\n  /** Gore.type（贴图 Gore_<type>.png） */\n  readonly goreId: number;\n  rotation = 0;\n  scale: number;\n  alpha = 0;\n  timeLeft = GORE_TIME;\n  sticky = true;\n  /** Gore.light（NewGore :1487-1490 仅 16/17 置 1；Update 尾段据此 AddLight） */\n  light = 0;\n  /** 贴图原始尺寸（碰撞盒与绘制基准；缺失回退 32） */\n  texW: number;\n  texH: number;\n  /** SpriteFrame 行（多帧竖条：1217/1218 等） */\n  row = 0;\n  private frameCounter = 0;\n\n  constructor(type: number, x: number, y: number, scale: number) {\n    super();\n    this.goreId = type;\n    this.scale = scale;\n    const tex = TEX[type];\n    this.texW = tex?.w ?? FALLBACK_TEX;\n    this.texH = tex?.h ?? FALLBACK_TEX;\n    this.x = x;\n    this.y = y;\n    this.resizeBox();\n  }\n\n  /** 碰撞盒 = min(texW,texH)*0.9*scale（Gore.cs:1000-1014） */\n  private resizeBox() {\n    const raw = Math.min(this.texW, this.texH) * 0.9 * this.scale;\n    this.w = Math.max(2, Math.floor(raw));\n    this.h = this.w;\n  }\n\n  /** 16/17 火苗族加性全亮（GetAlpha :1487-1490：Color(255,255,255,0) 预乘=加性） */\n  get additive(): boolean {\n    return this.goreId === 16 || this.goreId === 17;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * 光照贡献（Gore.Update 尾段 :1078-1101）：light>0 时以中心格 AddLight\n   * light*scale，16 号 B×0.3/G×0.8、17 号 G×0.6/R×0.3（衰减后的通道）。\n   * 由 Game 每帧消费（GorePiece 不持有 Lighting 引用）。\n   */\n  lightRGB(): { tx: number; ty: number; r: number; g: number; b: number } | null {\n    if (this.dead || this.light <= 0) return null;\n    let r = this.light * this.scale;\n    let g = r;\n    let b = r;\n    if (this.goreId === 16) { b *= 0.3; g *= 0.8; }\n    else if (this.goreId === 17) { g *= 0.6; r *= 0.3; }\n    const cx = this.x + (this.texW * this.scale) / 2;\n    const cy = this.y + (this.texH * this.scale) / 2;\n    return { tx: Math.floor(cx * PX), ty: Math.floor(cy * PX), r, g, b };\n  }\n\n  /** Gore.NewGore（Gore.cs:1306-1482）：池满/type 非法则弃；返回 null */\n  static newGore(\n    type: number, x: number, y: number, vx: number, vy: number, scale = 1, perfect = false,\n  ): GorePiece | null {\n    if (type < 0 || livePool.size >= MAX_GORE) return null;\n    const g = new GorePiece(type, x, y, scale);\n    g.vx = vx; g.vy = vy;\n    if (!perfect) {\n      // :1347-1348 随机扰动：vy -= Next(10,31)*0.1 ∈ [-3.0,-1.0]；vx += Next(-20,21)*0.1 ∈ [-2.0,2.0]\n      g.vy -= irand2(10, 31) * 0.1;\n      g.vx += irand2(-20, 21) * 0.1;\n    }\n    g.rotation = 0;\n    g.alpha = 0;\n    // :1339-1345 ChildSafety：童模开启 → 危险 gore 替换 11-13（scale 半随机、速度减半），\n    // 替换先于粘滞/时档/16/17/SpecialAI 分支（后续判定均按新 type）\n    let t = type;\n    if (!ChildSafety.disabled && ChildSafety.dangerous(t)) {\n      t = 11 + irand(3);\n      g.goreIdCompat(t);\n      g.scale = Math.random() * 0.5 + 0.5;\n      g.vx /= 2;\n      g.vy /= 2;\n      g.resizeBox();\n    }\n    // :1361-1374 粘滞与时档\n    if (NON_STICKY_TYPES.has(t)) {\n      g.sticky = false;\n    } else if (t >= 375 && t <= 377) {\n      g.sticky = false;\n      g.alpha = 100;\n    } else {\n      g.sticky = true;\n      g.timeLeft = GORE_TIME;\n    }\n    // :1487-1490 16/17 火苗族：alpha=100 + scale=0.7 + light=1\n    if (t === 16 || t === 17) {\n      g.alpha = 100;\n      g.scale = 0.7;\n      g.light = 1;\n      g.resizeBox();\n    }\n    // :1391-1398 多帧族（Frame=SpriteFrame(1,rows)，行 0 起；1218 首帧随机行在 Update 内）\n    if (FRAME_ROWS[t]) g.row = 0;\n    // :1423-1425 SpecialAI==1（帆降）：初速整体覆写为 ((rand-0.5)*3, rand*2π)——\n    // vy 兼作相位累加器，Gore_UpdateSail 据此走圆弧下落\n    if (SAIL_TYPES.has(t)) {\n      g.vx = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 3;\n      g.vy = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;\n    }\n    // :1442-1449（907-909 随机起始帧；本表未出现，保留语义）\n    if (t >= 907 && t <= 909) g.row = irand(3);\n    livePool.add(g);\n    return g;\n  }\n\n  /** ChildSafety 替换后的 type 回写（readonly 字段内部修正） */\n  private goreIdCompat(t: number): void {\n    (this as { goreId: number }).goreId = t;\n  }\n\n  /** 每固定 tick（Gore.Update，Gore.cs:313-1102） */\n  fixedUpdate(dt: number, game: GameHooks): void {\n    void dt;\n    if (this.dead) return;\n    const st = game.world.store;\n    const t = this.goreId;\n\n    // sticky 入口：越界消亡 + 速度钳 32（:319-329 / DeactivateIfOutsideOfWorld :297-311）\n    if (this.sticky) {\n      const tx = Math.floor(this.x / 16), ty = Math.floor(this.y / 16);\n      if (!st.inBounds(tx, ty)) { this.kill(); return; }\n      const sp = Math.hypot(this.vx, this.vy);\n      if (sp > 32) { this.vx *= 32 / sp; this.vy *= 32 / sp; }\n    }\n\n    // SpecialAI 7（1218）：UpdateLightningBunnySparks :244-262（switch :331-345 早退）\n    if (t === 1218) {\n      if (this.frameCounter === 0) {\n        this.frameCounter = 1;\n        this.row = irand(3); // Frame(1,3) 内随机行\n      }\n      this.timeLeft -= vanishSpeed(t);\n      if (this.timeLeft <= 0) { this.kill(); return; }\n      this.alpha = Math.round(255 - (255 * Math.max(0, this.timeLeft)) / 15);\n      this.x += this.vx;\n      this.y += this.vy;\n      if (this.alpha >= 255) this.kill();\n      return;\n    }\n\n    if (SAIL_TYPES.has(t)) { this.updateSail(game); return; }\n\n    // ---- 位移前物理（:352-769 链）----\n    if (isDampRibbon(t)) {\n      // :352-371 彩带阻尼\n      this.vx *= 0.98;\n      this.vy *= 0.98;\n      if (this.vy < this.scale) this.vy += 0.05;\n      if (this.vy > 0.1) this.rotation += this.vx > 0 ? 0.01 : -0.01;\n      this.vy += 0.2;                 // 默认重力支（:760-764）\n      this.rotation += this.vx * 0.05;\n    } else if (t === 16 || t === 17) {\n      // :546-556 火苗族独立支：0.01 收缩档（与血块 0.007 区分）\n      this.vy *= 0.98;\n      this.vx *= 0.98;\n      this.scale -= 0.01;\n      if (this.scale < 0.1) { this.scale = 0.1; this.alpha = 255; }\n      this.resizeBox();\n    } else if (SHRINK_TYPES.has(t)) {\n      // :535-556 血块收缩（0.007 档）\n      this.vy *= 0.98;\n      this.vx *= 0.98;\n      this.scale -= 0.007;\n      if (this.scale < 0.1) { this.scale = 0.1; this.alpha = 255; }\n      this.resizeBox();\n    } else {\n      // 默认支（:760-764）：重力 0.2 + 随 vx 翻滚\n      this.vy += 0.2;\n      this.rotation += this.vx * 0.05;\n    }\n\n    // ---- 碰撞/时档尾段（:929-1042）----\n    if (this.sticky) {\n      moveAndCollide(this, game.world, this.vx, this.vy);\n      if (this.vy === 0) {\n        // 落地摩擦（:1015-1029）：|vx|<0.01 归零\n        this.vx *= 0.97;\n        if (this.vx > -0.01 && this.vx < 0.01) this.vx = 0;\n      }\n      if (this.timeLeft > 0) this.timeLeft -= vanishSpeed(t);\n      else this.alpha += vanishAlpha(t);\n    } else {\n      // :1041 非粘滞：两倍速渐隐\n      this.alpha += 2 * vanishAlpha(t);\n      this.x += this.vx;\n      this.y += this.vy;\n    }\n\n    if (this.alpha >= 255) this.kill();\n  }\n\n  /** Gore_UpdateSail（Gore.cs:1231-1291）：相位下落；碰撞转落地滑移支 */\n  private updateSail(game: GameHooks): void {\n    const t = this.goreId;\n    if (this.vy < 0) {\n      // 落地滑移支（:1233-1261）：只推 velocity.X，不推进 position\n      let moveX = this.vx;\n      const size = Math.floor(Math.min(this.texW, this.texH) * 0.9 * this.scale);\n      const probe = { x: this.x, y: this.y, w: size, h: size, vx: moveX, vy: 0.6, onGround: false, hitWall: false, hitHead: false };\n      moveAndCollide(probe, game.world, moveX, 0.6);\n      moveX = probe.vx * 0.97;\n      if (moveX > -0.01 && moveX < 0.01) moveX = 0;\n      if (this.timeLeft > 0) this.timeLeft -= vanishSpeed(t);\n      else this.alpha += vanishAlpha(t);\n      this.vx = moveX;\n      if (this.alpha >= 255) this.kill();\n      return;\n    }\n    // 下落支（:1263-1290）：vy 兼作相位累加器；UnitY.RotatedBy(vy) = (−sin, cos)，Y 取 |·|\n    this.vy += Math.PI / 60;\n    const dx = -Math.sin(this.vy) * 2 * 2;\n    const dy = Math.abs(Math.cos(this.vy)) * 3 * 2;\n    const size = Math.floor(Math.min(this.texW, this.texH) * 0.9 * this.scale);\n    const body = { x: this.x, y: this.y, w: size, h: size, vx: dx, vy: dy, onGround: false, hitWall: false, hitHead: false };\n    moveAndCollide(body, game.world, dx, dy);\n    if (body.vx !== dx || body.vy !== dy) this.vy = -1; // 碰撞 → 落地支\n    this.x = body.x;\n    this.y = body.y;\n    this.rotation = Math.atan2(body.vy, body.vx) + Math.PI;\n    if (this.timeLeft > 0) this.timeLeft -= vanishSpeed(t);\n    else this.alpha += vanishAlpha(t);\n    if (this.alpha >= 255) this.kill();\n  }\n\n  private kill(): void {\n    if (this.dead) return;\n    this.dead = true;\n    livePool.delete(this);\n  }\n\n  /** Renderer 统一绘制（Main.DrawGore Main.cs:21712-21742：中心锚 + scale + rotation） */\n  draw(): void { /* 见 Renderer.drawGorePieces */ }\n\n  /** 多帧行数（1 = 整张贴图） */\n  get frameRows(): number {\n    return FRAME_ROWS[this.goreId] ?? 1;\n  }\n}\n\n/** 表项 → 具体 gore id（区间/候选均匀取值；微光变体取 idAlt；不可求值跳过） */\nfunction entryId(e: GoreSpawnEntry, s: GoreNpcSource): number | null {\n  if (e.id != null) return s.isShimmerVariant && e.idAlt != null ? e.idAlt : e.id;\n  if (e.idMin != null && e.idMax != null) return e.idMin + irand(e.idMax - e.idMin + 1);\n  if (e.ids?.length) return e.ids[irand(e.ids.length)];\n  return null;\n}\n\n/** rot 项（Unit 轴 × 系数，按 NPC rotation 旋转；'sd' 符号取 spriteDirection） */\nfunction rotOffset(terms: GoreRotTerm[], s: GoreNpcSource): { x: number; y: number } {\n  let x = 0, y = 0;\n  const rot = s.rotation ?? 0;\n  const cos = Math.cos(rot), sin = Math.sin(rot);\n  for (const t of terms) {\n    const sgn = t.s === 'sd' ? ((s.spriteDirection ?? 1) !== 1 ? 1 : -1) : t.s;\n    const k = t.k * (t.dim === 'w' ? s.w : t.dim === 'h' ? s.h : 1);\n    // (UnitX|UnitY)*sgn*k 旋转 rot：(x cosθ − y sinθ, x sinθ + y cosθ)\n    const bx = t.a === 'x' ? sgn * k : 0;\n    const by = t.a === 'y' ? sgn * k : 0;\n    x += bx * cos - by * sin;\n    y += bx * sin + by * cos;\n  }\n  return { x, y };\n}\n\n/** 表项 → 出生点（基准 = NPC 左上角；未编译回退原点） */\nfunction entryPos(e: GoreSpawnEntry, s: GoreNpcSource): { x: number; y: number } {\n  let x = s.x + (e.ox ?? 0) + (e.fx ?? 0) * s.w;\n  let y = s.y + (e.oy ?? 0) + (e.fy ?? 0) * s.h;\n  if (e.rwx) x += range01(e.rwx) * s.w;\n  if (e.rwy) y += range01(e.rwy) * s.h;\n  if (e.jx) x += range01(e.jx);\n  if (e.jy) y += range01(e.jy);\n  if (e.dx != null) x += e.dx * (s.direction ?? s.spriteDirection ?? 1);\n  if (e.dy != null) y += e.dy * (s.direction ?? s.spriteDirection ?? 1);\n  if (e.dc) { const d = disk(e.dc[0], e.dc[1]); x += d.x; y += d.y; }\n  if (e.rot) { const r = rotOffset(e.rot, s); x += r.x; y += r.y; }\n  return { x, y };\n}\n\n/** 表项 → 初始速度。语义 = NewGore 的 Velocity 实参整体替换（不含 NewGore 自带的\n *  随机扰动）：常量 > 均匀随机 > 椭圆盘/随机方向/rotation 向量 > NPC 速度×k >\n *  击退方向 > 继承 NPC 速度（缺省） */\nfunction axisVel(v: number | undefined, k: number | undefined, m: number | undefined,\n  a: number | undefined, hd: boolean, inherit: number, hitDir: number): number {\n  if (v != null) return v;\n  if (a != null) return uniform(m ?? 0, a);\n  if (k != null) return k * inherit;\n  if (hd) return hitDir;\n  return inherit;\n}\nfunction entryVel(e: GoreSpawnEntry, s: GoreNpcSource): { vx: number; vy: number } {\n  const hd = s.hitDir ?? 0;\n  if (e.dc) { const d = disk(e.dc[0], e.dc[1]); return { vx: d.x, vy: d.y }; }\n  if (e.de != null) {\n    // 均匀随机方向 × 定速（UnitY.RotatedByRandom(2π)*K / Transform((K,0), rot(rand*2π))）\n    const a = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;\n    return { vx: Math.cos(a) * e.de, vy: Math.sin(a) * e.de };\n  }\n  if (e.rv) {\n    const [k, flip] = e.rv;\n    let vx = Math.cos(s.rotation ?? 0) * k;\n    const vy = Math.sin(s.rotation ?? 0) * k;\n    if (flip % 2 === 1 && (s.spriteDirection ?? 1) === 1) vx = -vx;\n    return { vx, vy };\n  }\n  if (e.hdm) return { vx: range01(e.hdm) * hd, vy: axisVel(e.vy, e.ky, e.my, e.ay, false, s.vy, hd) };\n  return {\n    vx: axisVel(e.vx, e.kx, e.mx, e.ax, !!e.hd, s.vx, hd),\n    vy: axisVel(e.vy, e.ky, e.my, e.ay, false, s.vy, hd),\n  };\n}\n\n/** 表项 ai 门（原版 `ai[N] == V`）：不满足跳过 */\nfunction aiGateOk(e: GoreSpawnEntry, s: GoreNpcSource): boolean {\n  if (!e.ai?.length) return true;\n  return e.ai.every((g) => {\n    const v = g.slot === 0 ? s.ai0 : g.slot === 1 ? s.ai1 : g.slot === 2 ? s.ai2 : s.ai3;\n    return g.not ? v !== g.value : v === g.value;\n  });\n}\n\nconst cmp = (a: number, op: string | undefined, b: number): boolean => {\n  switch (op) {\n    case '==': return a === b;\n    case '!=': return a !== b;\n    case '>=': return a >= b;\n    case '<=': return a <= b;\n    case '>': return a > b;\n    case '<': return a < b;\n    default: return false;\n  }\n};\n\n/** RedHatSkeletronAdjustmentsEnabled（NPC.cs:67282）：35/33 看 ai[3]、36/32 看 localAI[3] */\nfunction redHatOk(s: GoreNpcSource): boolean {\n  const t = s.vanillaId;\n  if (t === 35) return s.ai3 === 1;\n  if (t === 33) return s.ai3 === 1;\n  if (t === 36 || t === 32) return (s.lai3 ?? 0) === 1;\n  return false;\n}\n\n/** 表项结构化守卫门（1:1 HitEffect 守卫语义）：不满足跳过该条 */\nexport function goreGatesOk(e: GoreSpawnEntry, s: GoreNpcSource): boolean {\n  if (!e.gates?.length) return true;\n  for (const g of e.gates) {\n    switch (g.k) {\n      case 'alt': if (!cmp(s.altTexture ?? 0, g.op, g.v ?? 0)) return false; break;\n      case 'sdir': if (!cmp(s.spriteDirection ?? 1, g.op, g.v ?? 0)) return false; break;\n      case 'dmg': if (!cmp(s.dmg ?? 9999, g.op, g.v ?? 0)) return false; break;\n      case 'shim': if ((!!s.isShimmerVariant) === !!g.not) return false; break;\n      case 'net': if (!cmp(goreNetMode, g.op, g.v ?? 0)) return false; break;\n      case 'rnd': if (!cmp(irand(g.n ?? 2), g.op, g.v ?? 0)) return false; break;\n      case 'redhat': if (!redHatOk(s)) return false; break;\n      case 'skip': return false;   // 提取器登记的不可求值守卫（保守恒假）\n      default: return false;\n    }\n  }\n  return true;\n}\n\n/** NewGore 之后的实例后处理（有序应用，提取自 HitEffect 同 type 块内的赋值流） */\nfunction applyPost(g: GorePiece, e: GoreSpawnEntry, s: GoreNpcSource): void {\n  for (const op of e.post ?? []) {\n    switch (op[0]) {\n      case 'vm': g.vx *= op[1]; g.vy *= op[1]; break;\n      case 'va': {\n        const lo = Math.min(op[2], op[3]), hi = Math.max(op[2], op[3]);\n        const v = lo + Math.random() * (hi - lo);\n        if (op[1] === 'x') g.vx += v; else g.vy += v;\n        break;\n      }\n      case 'vs': {\n        const f = op[1] as unknown as Record<string, number | number[] | GoreRotTerm[] | undefined>;\n        const vel = entryVel(f as GoreSpawnEntry, s);\n        g.vx = vel.vx; g.vy = vel.vy;\n        break;\n      }\n      case 'ss': g.scale = op[1]; g['resizeBox' as keyof GorePiece] ? void 0 : void 0; break;\n      case 'sm': {\n        const lo = Math.min(op[1], op[2]), hi = Math.max(op[1], op[2]);\n        g.scale *= lo + Math.random() * (hi - lo);\n        break;\n      }\n      case 'al': g.alpha = op[1] === 'npc' ? (s.alpha ?? 0) : op[1]; break;\n      case 'ro': g.rotation = op[1]; break;\n      case 'ps': {\n        const p = entryPos(op[1] as unknown as GoreSpawnEntry, s);\n        g.x = p.x; g.y = p.y;\n        break;\n      }\n      default: break;\n    }\n  }\n  // scale/alpha 变更后碰撞盒同步（resizeBox 为私有，借类型旁路一次性触发）\n  (g as unknown as { resizeBox(): void }).resizeBox();\n}\n\n/** 按表炸出某 NPC 的全部死亡 gore；add = 实体入桶回调。返回生成数 */\nexport function spawnDeathGore(s: GoreNpcSource, add: (p: GorePiece) => void): number {\n  if (s.vanillaId == null) return 0;\n  const list = deathGoreFor(s.vanillaId);\n  let n = 0;\n  for (const e of list) {\n    if (!aiGateOk(e, s)) continue;\n    if (!goreGatesOk(e, s)) continue;\n    const times = e.loop ?? 1;\n    for (let k = 0; k < times; k++) {\n      const type = entryId(e, s);\n      if (type == null || type < 0) continue; // expr/posExpr 留档条目（提取器不可静态求值）\n      const pos = entryPos(e, s);\n      const vel = entryVel(e, s);\n      const scale = e.scale ?? (e.scaleExpr === 'scale' ? s.vanillaScale : 1);\n      const g = GorePiece.newGore(type, pos.x, pos.y, vel.vx, vel.vy, scale, !!e.perfect);\n      if (g) { applyPost(g, e, s); add(g); n++; }\n    }\n  }\n  return n;\n}\n\n/** Enemy/Critter 死亡钩子：查表炸 gore（props 桶；表空 = 原版只出尘，返回 0）。\n *  hitDir = HitEffect 的 hitDirection（击退方向，`new Vector2(hitDirection, 0f)` 类表项用）；\n *  spriteDirection 等变体字段由来源 NPC 提供（缺省：spriteDirection=1 / altTexture=0 /\n *  isShimmerVariant=false / rotation=0 / dmg=9999——原版脚本化死亡调用约定） */\nexport function spawnNpcDeathGore(\n  npc: GoreNpcSource, game: GameHooks, hitDir?: number, spriteDirection?: number,\n): number {\n  const add = (game as unknown as {\n    entities?: { add?: (p: unknown, bucket: string) => void };\n  }).entities;\n  if (!add?.add) return 0;\n  const src: GoreNpcSource = {\n    ...npc,\n    hitDir: hitDir ?? npc.hitDir,\n    spriteDirection: spriteDirection ?? npc.spriteDirection ?? 1,\n    direction: npc.direction ?? spriteDirection ?? npc.spriteDirection ?? 1,\n    dmg: npc.dmg ?? 9999,\n  };\n  return spawnDeathGore(src, (p) => add.add!(p, 'props'));\n}\n"
}

 "new_string": "  private drawGorePiece(ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, p: GorePiece, z: number) {\n    const img = this.atlas!.ensureVImage(`vanilla/Gore_${p.goreId}.png`);\n    if (!img || !img.complete || img.naturalWidth === 0) return;\n    const rows = p.frameRows;\n    const fh = img.naturalHeight / rows;\n    const sh = Math.floor(fh);\n    const sw = img.naturalWidth;\n    // Gore.GetAlpha（:1486）：opacity = (255 - alpha)/255；\n    // 16/17 火苗（:1487-1490 返回 Color(255,255,255,0)）——XNA 预乘混合 A=0 即加性\n    // 全亮（1405 同处取场景光照 RGB，Gore.cs:1164-1174；此处从 1456）→ canvas 'lighter'\n    ctx.globalCompositeOperation = p.additive ? 'lighter' : 'source-over';\n    ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, (255 - p.alpha) / 255);\n    if (p.rotation === 0 && p.scale === 1) {\n      // 直绘路径（Main.cs:21706-21742 原点即贴图中心：落点 + 尺寸/2）\n      ctx.drawImage(img, 0, p.row * sh, sw, sh, p.x, p.y, sw, sh);\n      return;\n    }\n    ctx.save();\n    ctx.translate(p.x + (sw * p.scale) / 2, p.y + (sh * p.scale) / 2);\n    if (p.rotation) ctx.rotate(p.rotation);"
}

```
   *  翻滚随 vx 摩擦冻结）。 */
  private drawGorePieces(ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, cam: Camera, viewW: number, viewH: number) {
    if (!this.atlas) return;
    const pieces = liveGorePieces();
    if (!pieces.length) return;
    const z = cam.zoom;
    // 视区外剔除（扩半贴图防边缘闪断）
    const x0 = cam.x - viewW / 2 - 64, x1 = cam.x + viewW / 2 + 64;
    const y0 = cam.y - viewH / 2 - 64, y1 = cam.y + viewH / 2 + 64;
    ctx.save();
    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
    for (const p of pieces) {
      if (p.x < x0 || p.x > x1 || p.y < y0 || p.y > y1) continue;
      this.drawGorePiece(ctx, p, z);
    }
    ctx.restore();
    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
  }

```

所有工作已完成并经验证。以下是总结：

## 五个遗留项全部清零

**1. gates 拦截（110 条 → 结构化 131 条）** — `tools/extract-gore.mjs` 的 `parseGateClauses`+`classifyClause` 把守卫编译成 8 类结构化门：`alt`(altTexture)/`sdir`(spriteDirection)/`dmg`(HitEffect 实参)/`shim`(IsShimmerVariant)/`net`(Main.netMode)/`rnd`(Next(N)==V)/`redhat`(NPC.cs:67282)，循环相位门（`num709<3`）静态折算成 loop 次数。三个关键语义修复：
- **OR 型守卫** `altTexture==0 || type==106`：106 型免门且只豁免同层门（原先跨层误豁免，导致 redhat 门被吞）；
- **`NPCID.Sets.IsTownPet[type]` 静态展开**（NPCID.cs:4444）→ 城镇宠物 637-684 共 11 个 id 首次拿到 gore；
- **else 支取反**：`if (spriteDirection != -1){A} else {B}` 的 B 段自动带 `== -1` 门（npc 551 原先 21 块 → 现按朝向 10 块）。
运行时 `goreGatesOk` 逐条求值；来源字段 `altTexture/isShimmerVariant/spriteDirection/direction/rotation/lai3/dmg` 均为可选（默认值文档化，`dmg` 缺省 9999 = 原版脚本化死亡调用约定）；netMode 为模块常量（`setGoreNetMode`）。

**2. posExpr/velExpr（63/25 → 2/0）** — Vector2 局部赋值/突变流（vector19/20/24/25/9-11/2-3/5-6/30、center2，含 `v *= K`、`v.X *= -1f` 翻转累积）+ 项式代数（锚点/UnitX/Y×系数[×direction]/`.RotatedBy(rotation)`/NextVector2Circular/RandomVector2/局部分量引用/`<和式>×常数` 递归/线性求值器）。**荷兰人 491 全链编译**成 `rot:[{a,s:'sd',k,dim:'w'}]`；循环变量（num189/191/192、EoL 437 的 `728+num97`）按循环域逐值展开成固定 id。残留 2 条 posExpr = 肉山屏幕级尘幕（num594 依赖 `Main.screenPosition`，非 NPC 相对），已在 `_meta.notes` 列明。

**3. 实例后处理（259 条）** — 追踪 `int n = NewGore(...)` → `Main.gore[n].field` 及 `Gore g = Main.gore[n]` 别名链（含重绑），编译成有序 op：`vm/va/vs/ss/sm/al/ro/ps`，运行时 `applyPost` 按序应用。

**4. gore 16/17 语义查实 = 火苗非岩浆血**：NewGore 置 alpha=100/scale=0.7/light=1，独立 0.01 收缩支（原实现落入重力支已修），Update 尾段 AddLight 衰减通道（16=(1,0.8,0.3)、17=(0.3,0.6,1)），GetAlpha `Color(255,255,255,0)` 在 XNA 预乘混合下=加性全亮（1405 同处为取光照 RGB，注释标两版行号）。落地：`lightRGB()` + Game.ts 每帧点光消费 + Renderer `lighter` 加性绘制。

**5. ChildSafety** — SafeGore 338 id 全表嵌入，`ChildSafety.disabled` 默认 true（原版），开启后危险 gore 替换 11-13 落叶 + scale 半随机 + 速度减半，且替换先于粘滞/16-17/SpecialAI 分支（1:1 顺序）。
